### [1] [Rationally Enriched Chebyshev Trunk Bases for DeepONet Surrogates of High Péclet Entrance Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19658)
*Mingeun Choi,Satish Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于有理增强Chebyshev（REC）基函数的DeepONet代理模型，用于求解具有薄边界层的奇异摄动和高Péclet传输问题。REC基函数结合了Chebyshev多项式和AAA算法构造的有理函数，在多个测试问题上相比传统DeepONet和纯Chebyshev基DeepONet在预测温度、浓度等剖面时表现出更低的误差，并抑制了近壁振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异摄动和高Péclet传输问题的解剖面具有薄的局部边界层或壁面层，这类陡峭梯度特征难以用标准的多项式基函数（如Chebyshev多项式）高效表示，导致传统DeepONet在预测此类问题时精度不足或产生人为振荡。因此需要设计更适应这类解特征的主干网络基函数。

Method: 提出REC（rationally enriched Chebyshev）主干网络，将Chebyshev多项式字典元素与基于自适应Antoulas-Anderson（AAA）算法构造的有理字典元素相结合，构建DeepONet的主干网络。实验中在三个基准问题上（奇异摄动标量BVP、恒温壁热入口问题、吸热壁浓度入口问题）与vanilla DeepONet和纯Chebyshev主干DeepONet进行对比，每个问题执行五次独立训练。

Result: 在标量剖面对比中，REC-trunk DeepONet优于vanilla DeepONet，与Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet相当，但在摄动参数介于1.00×10^-4和1.78×10^-4之间时，相对Chebyshev-trunk可将剖面误差最多降低19.5%。在壁面法向温度和浓度剖面预测中，相对vanilla和Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet分别最多降低60.2%和32.2%的误差，并在Péclet数或质量传递Péclet数范围10^2至10^4内抑制了人工近壁振荡。

Conclusion: REC-trunk DeepONet通过结合Chebyshev多项式与AAA算法构造的有理函数，有效增强了DeepONet对具有薄边界层/壁面层特征的奇异摄动和高Péclet传输问题的近似能力，在保持与纯Chebyshev基相当性能的同时，在中低摄动参数区间和壁面剖面预测上展现出显著优势，并有效消除了近壁人工振荡。

Abstract: This study demonstrates a rationally enriched Chebyshev (REC) trunk for deep operator network (DeepONet) surrogate models of singularly perturbed and high-Péclet transport problems whose solution profiles are characterized by thin localized boundary or wall layers. The REC trunk combines Chebyshev polynomial dictionary elements with rational dictionary elements constructed using the adaptive Antoulas-Anderson (AAA) algorithm. Over five independent training runs, the resulting REC-trunk DeepONet is evaluated against a vanilla DeepONet and a Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet whose prescribed dictionary consists only of Chebyshev polynomials across three problems whose singular perturbation parameters are diffusion-to-advection ratios: a singularly perturbed scalar boundary-value problem (BVP), the thermal entrance problem with a prescribed wall temperature, and the concentration entrance problem with an absorbing wall. Across the held-out test profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet improves over the vanilla DeepONet and remains comparable to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet in predicting the scalar profile, with its clearest advantage over the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet appearing when the perturbation parameter lies between $1.00\times10^{-4}$ and $1.78\times10^{-4}$, where it reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $19.5\,\%$ relative to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet. In predicting the wall-normal temperature and concentration profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $60.2\,\%$ and $32.2\,\%$ relative to the vanilla and Chebyshev-trunk DeepONets, respectively, while suppressing artificial near-wall oscillations as the Péclet or mass-transfer Péclet number ranges from $10^{2}$ to $10^{4}$.

</details>


### [2] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督机器学习的直升机起飞重量估算模型，利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据，并遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准，实现了LSTM循环神经网络的机器学习需求、模型描述与实现验证，并证明其可在传统航电计算机上部署以支持机载告警等关键功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空安全关键领域（如机载告警）中需要准确的直升机起飞重量估算，但传统方法可能精度不足或适应性有限；同时机器学习在航空关键功能中的应用需要严格的适航保障流程，因此有必要开发一个符合监管框架（EASA概念文件及Eurocae ED-324）的机器学习模型及验证方法。

Method: 采用监督机器学习方法，构建基于长短期记忆（LSTM）递归神经网络的重量估算模型；利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集进行训练；提出一套完整的机器学习需求（ML Requirements）、机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description），并通过学习保证（learning assurance）流程与EASA概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准对齐，最终对实施的模型进行需求验证。

Result: 所开发的LSTM重量估算模型成功通过了机器学习需求的验证，并在传统遗留航电计算机上完成了部署演示，证明了该实现方案适用于在机载目标上运行关键功能（如机载告警）的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文将机器学习模型引入航空关键重量估算功能，通过遵循正规的学习保证流程和适航标准，实现了可部署于嵌入式航电平台的LSTM模型，为机器学习在安全关键航空应用中的落地提供了可行的路径和示范。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [3] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量——三角模糊重缩放距离(d_TR)，该度量将线性重缩放(LRE)直接集成到距离计算中，无需预先归一化即可处理异质尺度的模糊数据。论文形式化证明了d_TR满足度量性质，并具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策常涉及模糊不确定信息（尤其三角模糊数TFN），而现有距离度量假设所有数值处于同一尺度，在应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异质属性时需要进行额外的归一化预处理，这增加了计算复杂度并可能引入误差。

Method: 提出三角模糊重缩放距离(d_TR)，将线性重缩放(LRE)直接嵌入距离计算过程，在模糊数比较的同时完成归一化。通过严格的数学证明验证d_TR满足度量性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并证明其具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。

Result: 形式化证明了d_TR是一个真正的度量（metric），具备非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式；同时具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性；可结合权重向量对维度进行优先级排序。

Conclusion: d_TR作为一种无需预处理归一化即可处理异质模糊数据的距离度量，适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助等应用场景，为处理异质模糊数据提供了更为高效和稳健的距离度量工具。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [4] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在动态心电图领域缺乏高质量数据集和基准的问题，提出了Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态心电数据集和Holtercare-Bench基准，用于评估模型在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结上的表现，并通过实验发现现有MLLMs在超长病理序列处理上的显著性能差距及微调后的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在医疗应用中表现优异，但大多偏向静态图像或短期信号处理。动态心电图（ECG）作为关键的动态生理信号领域，模型因缺乏高质量数据集和基准，难以实现复杂的时间推理和诊断报告生成。因此亟需构建大规模动态心电多模态数据集和评估基准。

Method: (1) 构建Holtercare-23K数据集，包含788条临床Holter记录派生的22,980个QA对，创新性地采用信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；(2) 提出Holtercare-Bench多模态基准，从时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度评估模型；(3) 对主流MLLMs进行零样本评估，并对代表性模型进行微调实验。

Result: 零样本评估显示主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列时存在显著的性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后可获得大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了当前MLLMs在电生理学领域的局限性，并为长期医疗多模态大语言模型提供了基础性基准。项目开源地址为https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [5] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子-经典混合机器学习方法在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用，通过将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间并采用基于保真度的量子核，与经典SVM基线对比，发现量子核模型在片段组学数据上表现有竞争力，但在甲基化数据上经典SVM仍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量胸部CT肺癌筛查虽可降低死亡率，但受限于依从性和管理挑战。血液cfDNA生物标志物提供补充方案，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测困难。需要探索新的机器学习方法以更好地捕捉cfDNA中的非线性模式。

Method: 采用量子-经典混合机器学习框架：特征选择后使用20和40特征子集训练模型；通过角度特征映射和稠密角度特征映射将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间，并采用多种纠缠策略；基于精确态矢量模拟计算保真度量子核，与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，并与原始特征上训练的SVM模型进行对比。

Result: 片段组学数据上，多个20特征量子配置相比经典SVM基线提升了AUC，表明能有效捕捉cfDNA片段化的非线性结构；甲基化数据上经典SVM取得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力并在某些情况下提升了特异性；从20增加到40个特征并未一致提升性能，反而常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的一种有前景的技术路径得到支持，但仍需进一步优化编码和纠缠设计，且特征维度与模型性能之间无明显单调关系。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [6] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用数据集来增强LLM不确定性量化（UQ）的方法：基于已有的零样本不确定性分数和相似查询的正确性作为特征，构建简单分类器来预测LLM回答是否正确，在计算开销极小的前提下持续优于现有UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM不确定性量化方法（如口头化置信度、多次生成等）大多是零样本的，无需标注数据即可产生不确定性分数。然而在实际部署前，总需要在目标数据集上评估这些方法的性能。作者利用这一点，认为可以借助数据集来超越现有零样本方法的效果。

Method: 作者构建简单的分类器来预测LLM回答的正确性。分类器的特征包括：（1）现有的不确定性分数；（2）相似查询（similar queries）的正确性信息。该方法基于数据集进行训练，但计算开销极小，是一个轻量级的增强方案。

Result: 实验表明，利用目标数据集后，该方法在所有情况下一致地优于现有的不确定性量化分数方法，且几乎不增加计算开销。

Conclusion: 利用部署前的数据集来构建简单分类器，是一种成本低廉且有效的UQ增强方案，能够在实际应用中以最小开销获得更好的不确定性量化性能。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [7] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）的统一框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-vector乘积和子集干预等机制可解释性测量方法形式化为 y = Ax + w 的线性测量模型，并给出从低成本测量到逐步扩展测量族的实用流程，在多个模型（两状态HMM、GPT-2-small、Qwen-2.5-7B）上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-vector乘积、子集干预等）各自在不同访问假设下测量不同目标量，缺乏统一的数学框架来理解它们的共享结构、比较其成本效益并指导测量设计。

Method: 提出机制断层扫描框架：在选定基和干预族下，将测量形式化为 y = Ax + w（A为干预矩阵，x为目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基误设）。给出实用流程：从最低成本测量开始，在预期尺度的留出干预上测试，校准简单失配，当结构化残差存在时扩展测量族。

Result: 在双HMM模型中，控制误差随观测误差上升且目标改进可能掩盖干扰状态运动；前向仅访问下稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching用更少干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改进局部归因映射；提升测量和Hessian-vector乘积恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互。GPT-2-small IOI上Name Mover与Negative Name Mover交互是三个跨组对中最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上有限校准使加性拒绝响应映射足够，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描提供了一个统一语言来设计和评估机制测量，强调测量族的选择依赖基和目标，控制任务提供了严格的验证场景。结果表明：简单的加性模型在有限校准下可能已足够，提升测量族需由结构化残差驱动而非默认采用。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [8] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板（template）的加速技术在不同神经网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性，提出"联合稳定神经元"（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板重用效果差异，并提出了FastCert方法——自动在不同网络层间分布模板，在无加速潜力时完全弃用模板。在覆盖设计L0验证任务上，FastCert相比现有模板重用技术平均提升1.13倍速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证中，模板重用（proof sharing）已被证明能通过复用中间层抽象状态加速不完备验证技术，但其有效性在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下是否鲁棒尚不清楚。作者希望系统性地研究模板加速的适用范围及其局限性。

Method: 1) 对模板加速效果进行多维度系统性实验研究，覆盖不同架构、属性、数据集和训练方法；2) 提出"联合稳定神经元"这一新型度量指标来解释模板包含率（subsumption rate）的波动；3) 提出FastCert技术，自动在神经网络各层间分布模板，并在判定无加速潜力时完全跳过模板的使用。

Result: 实验表明模板包含率在不同场景下波动很大；部分情况下模板技术几乎不可能带来任何加速。在覆盖设计L0验证任务的大规模实验集中，FastCert相比现有模板重用技术实现了平均1.13倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 模板加速并非普遍适用，需要根据具体场景评估其潜力；联合稳定神经元指标可用于预判模板重用的效果；FastCert通过自适应模板分配和裁剪，在实际任务中取得了稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>

*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

TL;DR: 提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)框架，利用纵向扩散张量成像（DTI）和贝叶斯学习方法，将阿尔茨海默病进展建模为连续的、带不确定性的低维潜在变量（DCS评分），实现对疾病严重程度的连续定量评估，超越了传统离散诊断和临床评分的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病本质上是连续的生物学进程，但现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病随时间连续演化的特性。

Method: 提出DCP框架，一个纵向贝叶斯学习框架。将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜在变量，联合整合纵向观测数据与弱临床监督信号，并推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)来量化个体在疾病连续谱上的位置及相应不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上的大量实验表明，DCP一致性地优于代表性疾病进展方法。综合分析验证了DCS能准确表征疾病严重程度、具有强临床相关性、保留纵向疾病演化信息，并能预测未来的疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS为阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估提供了一种定量影像学表征，超越了传统诊断标签和临床评分的表达能力，有望成为疾病连续监测的新型生物标志物。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [10] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime，一个多分辨率框架，用于预测网络安全事件披露后金融时间序列的短期异常损失。该框架结合长期市场上下文、短期事件前动态和事件元数据，并通过动态对比学习缓解稀疏监督问题，在真实数据集SECURE上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件等通过网络传播的冲击会突然破坏金融时间序列并导致异常损失。现有时间序列预测模型聚焦于趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理稀有、异质的外部事件，且高影响事件稀疏和背景市场噪声进一步加大了挑战。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，包含长期市场上下文、短期事件前动态和事件元数据三个维度。核心创新包括：1) 事件融合模块，将时间序列表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关近期市场模式；2) 动态对比学习目标，在训练期间构建事件和时间序列感知的正负样本对，缓解稀疏监督问题。此外构建了SECURE数据集，将网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化/LLM派生语义特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估计事件后财务损失方面持续优于最新的时间序列和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其表示更具事件敏感性、对不完整元数据更具鲁棒性，并在网络安全披露后的短期市场影响估计上更具可解释性。

Conclusion: EventTime证明了将事件元数据与多分辨率时间序列建模结合、并通过对比学习应对稀疏监督，能有效解决事件条件影响预测问题，为网络安全披露引发的金融异常损失估计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [11] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用LLM的上下文学习能力，将表格数据的正常训练样本转换为统计摘要、因果依赖和原型聚类信息，通过提示词驱动代码生成构建评分引擎，无需微调即可实现表格数据异常检测，在24个数据集上超越15个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本往往体现为跨特征依赖关系的违反而非简单的边际偏差。现有方法依赖几何或重建信号，而基于LLM的方法主要通过正常样本微调或生成合成异常，尚未充分利用LLM的上下文学习能力来从结构化的正常状态知识中推导异常检测逻辑。

Method: LLM-Detector将正常训练数据转化为三类知识：统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，组织成提示词用于代码生成。生成的评分引擎从三个维度计算异常分数：统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常程度，综合得出每个测试样本的异常分数。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上，与15个SOTA基线对比，LLM-Detector在混合类型和纯连续数据设置中均表现出一致的性能提升。同时无需LLM微调或神经网络训练，大幅降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector证明了LLM的上下文学习和代码生成能力可以直接用于结构化、提示条件化的评分合成，实现无需训练的高效表格异常检测，具备实际部署的实用性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [12] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过3,933次受控实验研究机器学习系统在概念漂移下的重训练策略，发现最关键的决策不是重训练策略本身，而是模型是否支持增量学习。对于支持增量更新的线性在线学习器，任何策略与不重训练基线在54组配对比较中均无实际显著差异；若不支持增量更新，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于响应式策略，而响应式策略仅在循环漂移下有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但从业者缺乏关于何时重训练的原则性指导。重训练成本高昂、预算有限，且训练和部署延迟会导致陈旧模型在数据持续漂移期间继续服务。现有研究缺乏在统一系统模型下对不同重训练策略的系统性对比。

Method: 构建统一的系统模型，显式建模重训练预算和训练加部署延迟；对比三种实用模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发重训练）与不重训练基线；在3,933次实验运行中覆盖三种漂移机制、三种预算水平、最多五种延迟水平、三个数据集和两种学习模式进行受控实验。

Result: 增量学习是关键因素：对于支持逐样本增量更新的线性在线学习器，即使在极端延迟下，没有任何策略与不重训练基线存在实际显著差异（54组配对比较均无显著差异）；而不支持增量更新时，策略选择导致15-55个百分点的事后漂移准确率差异，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两种响应式策略，响应式策略仅在循环漂移下保持优势。研究还发现响应式策略的系统性失败模式，以及一个延迟-预算队列交互效应会静默地将有效重训练预算减半。

Conclusion: 选择合适的重训练策略前应优先考虑部署模型是否支持增量学习；若不支持，简单周期性重训练通常是最稳健的选择；研究揭示了响应式策略的失败机制和延迟-预算交互陷阱，并发布了完整的模拟器、数据管线和逐次运行工件以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [13] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeltaMomentum，将方向感知嵌入动量更新本身，利用线性层梯度的键值结构，用delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向按其出现频率被遗忘。作为任何优化器动量缓冲的即插即用替代品，在预训练中显著减少达到相同验证损失的步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化器的动量是梯度指数移动平均（EMA），对所有方向以固定速率遗忘。但深度网络训练中输入的梯度方向高度各向异性，少数方向频繁被查询而多数方向很少出现。现有方法通过在缓冲外包裹额外处理来应对这种各向异性，但动量更新规则本身未改变。

Method: 利用线性层梯度可分解为输入（作为key）和输出侧误差（作为value）这一键值结构，使用规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向以出现频率决定遗忘速率。证明了其动量有效性、无需矩阵逆的输入侧曲率校正，以及对固定和漂移最优点的陈旧方向清除速度优于EMA。额外计算量仅为gated-MLP块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，无持久内存开销。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW在67M规模下比AdamW减少最多46.39±4.32%的训练步数达到相同验证损失，370M规模下减少22.12±0.80%，1B规模上在Chinchilla最优预算下增益持续。Muon基线在同一协议下表现不及DeltaAdamW。SGD、ResNet-18和ViT-Tiny在CIFAR-10上同样有增益。训练诊断确认了预测机制：更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过在动量更新规则中内建方向感知，而非在事后包裹额外处理，有效解决了梯度各向异性问题。其系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移，计算开销可控，作为动量缓冲的即插即用替代品在多种优化器、模型和数据集上均展现一致的性能增益。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [14] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种操作能力层级和Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC)框架,用于检验多模态世界模型中不同模态表示是否承载相同的可执行语义,以及在新的动作组合下该语义是否仍然保持。实验显示,在Cluster Haptic数据集上,音频和加速度表示在响应空间中比错误表面对近了4.5倍;在受控弹塑性系统中,预注册预算下堆栈被拒绝,而收敛预算下融合提升了性能,14/16的已注册检查通过。作者将属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行分离为四个独立、可分别测试的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态世界模型探针无法确定不同传感器是否承载相同的可执行语义,也无法验证该语义在新动作组合下是否存活。需要一种操作性的能力检验框架来区分这些维度。

Method: 引入操作能力层级和DBOSC框架,核心是检验独立训练的多模态编译器是否能在外推证据上互换地进入冻结响应图谱。实验包括Cluster Haptic上的音频和加速度跨模态验证,以及受控弹塑性系统中有序执行、融合闭包、非可辨识性等测试。

Result: 在Cluster Haptic上,同一未见表面的音频和加速度表示比错误表面对近了4.5倍,且19个未见过表面全部成立。弹性塑性系统中,预注册预算下堆栈被拒绝(冻结执行器无法推进),收敛预算下秩三图谱可执行程序(oracle NMSE 0.18),融合优于单模态,14/16注册检查通过。两个失败源于融合信息矩阵的对角限制与完整矩阵性能相当。整程序发射执行器比实体盲预测器差38倍。

Conclusion: 压缩和融合本身无法确定未见过的组合法则(非可辨识性结果)。属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行是四个不同且可分别测试的能力成就。通过门控是执行器的性质,而非图谱的性质。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习研究往往只关注端点精度或声称量子优势，缺乏对学习内部动力学的深入理解。作者旨在揭示混合量子模型与传统经典模型在优化过程和核几何上的本质差异，探讨“不同的学习轨迹能否产生相同泛化”这一核心问题，而不急于宣称通用量子优势。

Method: 采用紧凑混合量子预测模型与结构对齐的经典基准进行对比。使用平稳谐波混合与非平稳啁啾两类基准，控制频谱复杂度和数据可用性。通过经验神经正切核（NTK）框架分析核-目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度及训练损失等动力学指标。同时设置傅里叶增强经典基线和受控的重复编码消融实验，以隔离架构因素对优化和核几何的影响。

Result: 经典模型表现出更强的早期目标对齐；混合模型整体上核谱集中度更低、核漂移更小。尽管优化几何明显不同，两种架构在评估范围内达到相似的保持集性能。混合模型仅用125个参数（经典基线281个），且在15/18种频率条件下更早达到验证选择检查点。傅里叶增强经典基线无法复现混合模型的训练行为，而重复编码消融证实了嵌入策略会系统性地改变优化与核几何。

Conclusion: 等价的泛化能力可以从本质不同的学习轨迹中涌现，单个NTK诊断指标并非验证收敛的单调预测器。研究不主张通用量子优势，而是强调架构依赖的学习行为可能被端点精度指标所掩盖，为量子机器学习提供了更细致的评估视角。

Abstract: We characterize the learning dynamics of a compact hybrid quantum forecasting model through comparison with a structurally aligned classical baseline. Using stationary harmonic-mixture and nonstationary chirp benchmarks with controlled spectral complexity and data availability, we analyze empirical Neural Tangent Kernel dynamics through kernel-target alignment, kernel drift, spectral concentration, and training loss. The classical model exhibits stronger early target alignment, whereas the hybrid model generally develops a less concentrated kernel spectrum and smaller kernel drift. Despite these distinct optimization geometries, both architectures attain similar held-out performance across the evaluated regimes. Notably, the hybrid model uses 125 trainable parameters compared with 281 for the classical baseline and reaches its validation-selected checkpoint earlier in 15 of 18 frequency conditions. A Fourier-augmented classical baseline does not reproduce the observed training behavior, while a controlled re-uploading ablation shows that repeated encoding systematically modifies both optimization and kernel geometry. These results demonstrate that comparable generalization can emerge from substantially different learning trajectories and that individual NTK diagnostics do not provide monotonic predictors of validation convergence. Rather than claiming a general quantum advantage, the study identifies architecture-dependent learning behavior that is masked by endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [16] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出4MAS（四模块清醒/睡眠）宏架构，借鉴动物记忆巩固的三个关键概念——经验回放、REM睡眠和大脑半球不对称性，使机器学习模型具备持续学习能力。在Split-MNIST、Split-Fashion-MNIST和Split-CIFAR-100上分别达到98.3%、84.9%和29.29%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习算法在持续学习中面临灾难性遗忘问题，需在保留已有知识与适应新数据之间权衡；当原始训练数据因存储、成本或隐私限制不可用时，全量重训练不切实际。而生物系统能够持续学习而不遗忘，因此需要从生物机制中汲取灵感，构建更具鲁棒性的持续学习框架。

Method: 提出4MAS宏观架构，包含四个模块：两个非对称半球各自配备长期和短期记忆机制，模拟大脑半球不对称性；引入经验回放机制保留关键历史样本；在两个增量学习任务之间引入模拟REM睡眠阶段以促进记忆巩固。通过在清醒阶段学习、睡眠阶段巩固的方式实现持续学习。

Result: 在Split-MNIST、Split-Fashion-MNIST和Split-CIFAR-100数据集上分别取得98.3%、84.9%和29.29%的准确率，达到竞争性水平。

Conclusion: 受生物记忆巩固机制启发的4MAS架构展示了结合清醒/睡眠学习周期与非对称半球设计在缓解灾难性遗忘方面的可行性，为持续学习系统提供了高层次的生物启发式设计框架。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [17] [Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19540)
*Yara Bahram,Zahra Dehghani,Mélodie Desbos,Eric Granger,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MeanFlow-Transfer (MF-T)和Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow (CAMF)两个方法，统一解决扩散/流模型在新领域上的少数据适应和多步采样加速两个难题。MF-T将异构源模型的输出映射到统一的平均速度（MeanFlow）表示，通过源权重初始化并用目标域上的MF目标函数优化，实现跨异构模型统一的自适应与加速；CAMF作为后训练阶段，将连续对抗流模型从瞬时速度扩展到MF的有限区间平均速度，通过对比真实与预测区间端点间学习势能的变化来恢复细节。实验表明在24个适配任务上（4个ImageNet预训练源模型适配5个目标领域），MF-T+CAMF在最多125×更少的函数评估次数下匹配或超过微调教师模型的FID和FDD，CAMF平均将MF-T少步FID改善29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在两方面的局限：一是将预训练扩散/流模型适配到新领域时，其昂贵的多步采样问题未被解决，而已有的加速方法受限于源模型的参数化方式（ε、x、v或u），异质预训练模型缺乏统一的加速目标；二是对抗精炼虽在少步生成中有效，但仅针对瞬时速度的流模型公式化，无法应用于MeanFlow模型预测的有限区间平均速度。

Method: 提出两个组成部分：(1) MeanFlow-Transfer (MF-T)：将异构源模型的输出映射为统一的平均速度表示，从源权重初始化MF生成器，并在目标域上优化MF目标函数，在单一训练循环内同时完成模型适配和加速，适用于广泛的预训练模型。(2) Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow (CAMF)：后训练阶段，将连续对抗流模型从瞬时速度扩展到MF的有限区间平均速度，通过比较真实与预测区间端点的学习势能变化来恢复MF回归平均掉的高频细节，在区间趋于零时退化为瞬时标准。

Result: 将四个基于ImageNet的源模型（DiT-ε、SiT-v、JiT-x、iMF-u）适配到五个目标领域共20个任务，MF-T+CAMF在最多125×更少的NFE下匹配或超过微调教师模型的FID和FDD；CAMF平均将MF-T的少步FID改善了29%。

Conclusion: MF-T统一了异构预训练模型的适配与加速，CAMF将对抗精炼有效扩展到平均速度表示，两者结合在少数据新领域上实现了高质量少步生成，并验证了广泛适用性（覆盖四类参数化的源模型和多个目标域）。

Abstract: Training fast generators on new domains with limited data remains challenging for two reasons. First, adapting a pretrained diffusion or flow model to a new domain leaves its costly multi-step sampling unaddressed, and existing acceleration methods are tied to the source parameterization--$ε$, $x$, $v$, or $u$--leaving heterogeneous pretrained models with no common acceleration target. Second, while adversarial refinement is proven effective for few-step quality, it is formulated only for instantaneous-velocity flows, not for the finite-interval average velocities that MeanFlow (MF) models predict. We address both problems. We propose MeanFlow-Transfer, which maps heterogeneous source outputs into a shared velocity representation, uses it to initialize an MF generator from the source weights, and optimizes an MF objective on the target domain. This unifies adaptation and acceleration in a single training loop across a broad range of pretrained models. We then introduce Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow, a post-training stage that extends continuous adversarial flow models from instantaneous velocities to MF's finite-interval average velocities. CAMF contrasts changes in a learned potential between real and predicted interval endpoints, recovering fine detail that MF regression averages away, and reduces to the instantaneous criterion in the vanishing-interval limit. Adapting four ImageNet-based source models--DiT ($ε$), SiT ($v$), JiT ($x$), iMF ($u$)--to five target domains, MF-T with CAMF matches or exceeds the fine-tuned teacher in FID and FDD at up to $125\times$ fewer Neural Function Evaluations (NFEs), while CAMF improves MF-T's few-step FID by $29\%$ on average.

</details>


### [18] [DraftFM: A FoundationModel for Day-Zero Drafting in Magic: The Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19568)
*Brian Ward*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DraftFM是一个离散选择策略模型，用于万智牌"日零"（day-zero）环境下的轮抽选择预测——即在新的补充包发布时，人类轮抽日志尚不存在，但卡牌列表已经公开。模型仅使用每张卡的公开记录（775维：结构化特征+固定文本嵌入），不含卡牌身份、系列身份或使用统计数据，因此能对未见过的卡牌与熟悉卡牌使用相同机制进行评分。该网络仅有160万参数，在149百万人类选择（来自29个系列）上训练，在3个完全保留的系列上达到50.8%、60.4%和56.7%的top-1一致性（开盘选择随机机会约7%）。用全部32个已观测系列重新拟合后，模型为当时尚未发布的"哈比人"系列生成卡牌排名，在系列于MTG Arena可轮抽前约36小时发布（附加密溯源证明），该排名与六位独立专家评审之间的一致性程度近似于专家彼此间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督式轮抽模型依赖已有人类轮抽日志来训练，但在新扩展包发布时（day-zero），完整卡牌列表已公开而轮抽日志尚不存在。这使得现有模型无法应对新系列。作者旨在直接研究这种"日零"场景，构建一个不需要任何系列特定数据就能预测未见过卡牌选择质量的模型。

Method: DraftFM是一个离散选择策略网络，对当前卡包中可用卡牌逐张评分，以已轮抽的牌池和轮抽状态为条件。每张卡牌以冷冻的775维特征向量表示，由公开卡牌记录的结构化特征和固定文本嵌入组成，模型不包含卡牌身份、系列身份或使用率统计。模型架构仅160万参数，在149百万人类选择上训练，并在三个被完全剔除的系列上进行留出评估。随后用全部32个系列重拟合，用于预测当时未发布的"哈比人"系列的卡牌排名，并在发布前约36小时以加密溯源方式公开。

Result: 在三个完全保留的系列上，模型top-1预测一致性分别达到50.8%、60.4%和56.7%，而开盘选择时均匀随机的基线约7%。针对未发布的"哈比人"系列生成的密封排名与六位独立专家评审的一致性程度，大致相当于这六位专家彼此之间的一致性水平。

Conclusion: DraftFM证明了在完全没有新系列过往人类选择数据的情况下，仅凭公开卡牌记录即可对新系列轮抽选择做出与人类专家水平相当的质量排名预测。该模型支持"日零"场景下的前瞻性部署，且作者承诺将对实际结果的评估作为后续研究发表。

Abstract: Drafting a new Magic: The Gathering expansion begins before any pick from it has been observed: the complete card list is public, but the draft logs that supervised pick models train on do not yet exist. We study this day-zero regime directly. DraftFM is a discrete-choice policy that scores exactly the cards available in the current pack, conditioned on the drafted pool and the state of the draft. Every card enters as a frozen 775-dimensional function of its public card record, structured features and a fixed text embedding, with no card identities, set identities, or usage statistics anywhere in the model, so an unseen card is scored by the same machinery as a familiar one. A 1.6-million-parameter network fitted on 149 million human picks from 29 expansions predicts held-out picks in three expansions withheld in their entirety, reaching 50.8%, 60.4%, and 56.7% top-1 agreement, where uniform chance at the opening pick is about 7%. Refitted on all 32 observed expansions, the same architecture produced a card ranking for the then-unreleased set The Hobbit, sealed with its complete cryptographic provenance and published roughly 36 hours before the set became draftable on MTG Arena. The sealed ranking agrees with six independent expert reviewers roughly as much as those reviewers agree with one another. Evaluation against realized outcomes is committed to a follow-on note, whatever it shows.

</details>


### [19] [A Two-Stage Time-Aware Transformer for Short-Horizon AECOPD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19578)
*Dongyang Wang,Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，直接在家庭呼吸机近七天的原始压力和流量波形上操作，第一阶段分类高危患者严重急性加重风险，第二阶段回归估计距事件发生剩余天数。实验显示分类器F1=0.91，回归模型RMSE=1.00天，R²=0.76，为临床提供早期预警和可操作的提前时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD恶化迅速，及时预测是临床重点。现有机器学习方法依赖零散的临床变量，存在延迟，难以适用于家庭监护场景。虽然家用呼吸机提供低延迟、近连续的呼吸状态记录，但现有基于呼吸机的方法要么压缩波形为手工特征，要么仅聚焦二分类风险判断，未解决事件发生时机的问题。

Method: 提出两阶段框架，直接处理最近七天家用呼吸机使用期间的原始压力和流量波形。第一阶段使用分类模型识别有严重急性加重高风险的患者；第二阶段使用回归模型估计距离事件发生的剩余天数。

Result: 两阶段模型在风险分类和时间-事件估计任务上均优于传统基线模型。选定的第一阶段分类器取得F1=0.91；第二阶段回归模型取得RMSE=1.00天，R²=0.76。

Conclusion: 该两阶段框架能同时提供早期预警和精确的提前时间估计，帮助临床医生在严重急性加重发生前获得可操作的时间窗口，提升了家庭监护场景下AECOPD预测的实用价值。

Abstract: Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) can worsen rapidly, making timely prediction a clinical priority. Most existing machine learning approaches rely on episodically collected clinical variables, introducing delays that limit their practical utility in home monitoring settings. Home ventilators offer a lower-latency alternative, producing a near-continuous record of respiratory status during daily use. However existing ventilator-based approaches either compress the waveform into handcrafted features or focus primarily on binary risk classification, leaving the timing of an impending event unresolved. In this paper, we present a two-stage framework that operates directly on raw pressure and flow waveforms from the most recent seven days of home ventilator use. The first-stage classification model identifies patients at high risk of a severe exacerbation. The second-stage regression model then estimates how many days remain before the event occurs. Our experimental results demonstrate that the two-stage model outperforms traditional baseline models on both risk classification and time-to-event estimation, with our selected Stage 1 classifier achieving F1 = 0.91 and our Stage 2 regression model achieving RMSE = 1.00 days and R^2 = 0.76, giving clinicians both an early warning and actionable lead time before a severe exacerbation occurs.

</details>


### [20] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从信息几何与复几何的视角研究复杂参数化神经网络的损失景观，提出在Kähler信息度量下利用Wirtinger Hessian和自然梯度下降进行优化，并重点探讨Calabi-Yau信息流形上由曲率条件引发的理论保证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习的优化理论主要基于欧几里得或黎曼几何框架，而复杂参数化网络（如复值网络）的损失景观具有更丰富的几何结构。作者希望从信息流形的视角，利用复几何工具（如Kähler度量、Wirtinger Hessian、Calabi-Yau流形）来建立新的优化保证，并揭示负曲率对损失景观的破坏性影响。

Method: 论文采用信息论与复几何交叉的方法：1) 通过Wirtinger Hessian在log-likelihood势能上定义Kähler信息度量；2) 使用自然梯度下降（用逆度量缩放的损失梯度）使下降路径保持在全纯切丛中；3) 在非紧致Calabi-Yau信息流形上，利用楔积无处消失的全纯形式与Kähler形式的top exterior product关系导出常数行列式条件；4) 分析截曲率与负定Ricci曲率对损失景观的影响。

Result: 论文发现：1) 在固定行列式条件下，度量若在特征值容差内近似低秩，会导致blow-up效应；2) 负截曲率会破坏损失景观的结构，并可与负定Ricci曲率相互关联；3) 负曲率与消逝曲率是神经网络理论保证失败的模式，与初始化渐近行为紧密相关。

Conclusion: 该研究将复几何与深度学习优化理论融合，建立了Calabi-Yau信息流形框架下的损失景观分析方法，揭示了几何曲率条件（尤其是负曲率）在神经网络训练动力学中的核心作用，为理解和改进复值网络的优化行为提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>

Abstract: While entropy regularization is widely used to stabilize and accelerate Natural Policy Gradient methods, its ability to yield faster convergence rates for the unregularized objective remains underexplored. Existing analyses often rely on double-loop architectures and invoke a linear entropy penalty. To bridge the gap between theory and practice, we analyze a single-loop, entropy-regularized Natural Actor-Critic algorithm under compatible linear function approximation. By training an uncentered critic, our critic tracking can remain stable even as the training policy approaches determinism and the Fisher information matrix degenerates. We focus on two primary regimes for the optimization landscape: a Stochastic Regime, where we fuse coupled actor-critic updates into a joint Lyapunov recurrence, and a Deterministic Regime, where we pivot to a Policy Mirror Descent framework to circumvent the collapse of Euclidean geometry. By exploiting a positive Minimal Action Gap in the unregularized Markov decision process, we introduce an Exponential Translation mechanism that maps the regularized gap to the unregularized one up to an exponentially decaying tail. By tuning the fixed temperature, our algorithm achieves accelerated unregularized convergence rates, up to approximation-error terms: $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1})$ in the Stochastic Regime, and $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$ for the average iterate alongside $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1/3})$ for the last iterate in the Deterministic Regime. Here, $T_{total}$ denotes the total number of stochastic critic updates (or Monte Carlo rollouts). Furthermore, in the tabular setting, our positive-action-gap analysis yields a $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$ average-iterate rate, surpassing the $\mathcal{O}(T_{total}^{-1/2})$ worst-case statistical barrier that applies without a positive action margin.

</details>

Conclusion: 单独有效的PINN干预之间存在非线性的相互作用，更复杂的训练方案并不必然更好。技术的可组合性需要系统性评估，恰当的少量技巧组合可能比堆砌更多技巧更有效。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) embed governing partial differential equations directly into the training loss, offering a promising alternative to costly CFD solvers for unsteady flows. Yet the growing list of techniques proposed to improve PINN training is typically validated one at a time, leaving open whether these techniques actually compose. We study this question in depth on the DFG/Schafer-Turek unsteady cylinder wake benchmark. In isolation, nearly every technique performs no better than an untreated baseline. However, combining periodic (SIREN) activations with causal weighting unlocks a previously inaccessible regime, reconstructing velocity and pressure fields to within 4.1% average relative L2 error against an OpenFOAM reference solution. Adding further techniques instead causes catastrophic performance degradation, demonstrating that individually effective PINN interventions can interact nonlinearly and that more elaborate training recipes are not necessarily better.

</details>

*Yi-Shan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出一个广泛使用的加权自归一化浓度不等式（用于折扣最小二乘估计器的非平稳问题中的时间一致保证）存在错误：通过一个标量高斯反例证明其声称的有界半径以概率1被跨越；作者找出证明错误（不同终端时间使用不同高斯混合分布，固定时间混合不构成超鞅），并证明该不等式仅在固定确定性时间有效，同时给出有限与无限时域的修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自归一化浓度不等式是bandit和强化学习分析的标准工具，其加权扩展形式被广泛用于非平稳问题中的折扣最小二乘估计器的时间一致保证。如果该不等式存在缺陷，将影响下游众多基于此的分析结果，因此有必要严格审视并修正该问题。

Method: 作者通过一个固定参数的简单标量高斯反例，构造性地证明该不等式声称的有界半径以概率1被跨越。随后，针对固定折扣和正则化参数，利用形式化下界分析证明：当δ≤1/2且T/δ充分大时，任何在条件亚高斯模型类上均匀有效的确定性任意时刻边界在某个时刻必须至少达到R√log(T/δ)量级（对非递减边界则在时刻T达到该量级）。同时识别出证明中的逻辑错误——不同终端时间对应的混合分布不同，导致固定时间混合无法构成单一超鞅，停时论证无法修复此失效。最后证明加权不等式在每个固定确定性时间仍然有效，并给出有限时域与无限时域的修正。

Result: 证明了原加权自归一化不等式的时间一致保证是无效的（精确反例+下界量级），定位了证明中的超鞅构造错误，同时证明该不等式在固定确定性时刻仍成立，并提供了有效的有限/无限时域修正版本，讨论了其对下游分析的影响。

Conclusion: 该加权自归一化浓度不等式的原始声明（时间一致均匀保证）不成立，但其固定时间版本有效；研究者在使用该工具进行bandit和强化学习分析时需采用修正后的边界或注意时间一致性的失效风险。

Abstract: Self-normalized concentration inequalities are standard tools in bandit and reinforcement-learning analyses. A widely used weighted extension claims an analogous time-uniform guarantee for discounted least-squares estimators in non-stationary problems. A simple scalar Gaussian counterexample with a fixed parameter shows that the claimed bounded radius is crossed with probability one. For fixed discount and regularization parameters, we further show that, when $δ\leq1/2$ and $T/δ$ is sufficiently large, any deterministic anytime boundary valid uniformly over the stated conditionally sub-Gaussian model class must be at least of order $R\sqrt{\log(T/δ)}$ at some time by horizon $T$; for nondecreasing boundaries, this order is required at time $T$. We identify the proof error: different terminal times use different Gaussian mixing distributions, so the fixed-time mixtures do not form one supermartingale, and the stopping-time argument does not repair this failure. Finally, we show that the weighted inequality remains valid at each fixed deterministic time, give valid finite- and infinite-horizon corrections, and discuss consequences for downstream analyses.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍DeltaML-Bench，一个包含48个源自研究论文任务的基准，用于评估自主机器学习实验智能体在真实不完美开源仓库中改进已有基线的能力。实验表明，基于搜索的ARG脚手架相比标准模块化智能体能显著提升GPT-5的成功率（从9.4%提升至33.9%，在2x12h配置下达49.0%），同时有效消除了规格博弈行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习实验基准未能完全捕捉真实条件：异构仓库导航、训练pipeline修复以及现实计算约束下的候选改进评估。需要一个更贴近实际场景的基准来准确评估自主智能体在机器学习实验中的能力。

Method: 构建DeltaML-Bench基准，包含48个来自研究论文的任务，要求智能体在不完美的开源仓库中改进已发表的基线。评估两种模型（GPT-5和Claude Sonnet 4）与两种脚手架方案（标准Modular agent和基于搜索的ARG）在两种计算资源分配（4x6h和2x12h）下的表现。

Result: 在4x6h分配下，ARG将GPT-5的单次运行成功率从9.4%提升至33.9%；在2x12h分配下，GPT-5 ARG达到49.0%的成功率。Modular配置的规格博弈率高达47.9%，而ARG配置未观察到任何规格博弈行为。

Conclusion: 脚手架设计与完整性检查是部署自主机器学习实验智能体时的重要考量因素。ARG脚手架在提升成功率和防止规格博弈方面均优于标准模块化设计，为未来自主ML实验代理的开发提供了方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agents for machine learning experimentation must navigate heterogeneous repositories, repair training pipelines, and evaluate candidate improvements under realistic compute constraints. Existing benchmarks only partially capture these conditions. We introduce DeltaML-Bench, a benchmark comprising 48 tasks sourced from research papers that require agents to improve published baselines within imperfect, open-source repositories. We evaluate GPT-5 and Claude Sonnet 4 with a standard Modular agent and a search-based ARG scaffolding. In the 4 x 6h allocation, ARG raises GPT-5's per-run success rate from 9.4% to 33.9%; in the 2 x 12h allocation, GPT-5 ARG reaches 49.0%. Modular configurations exhibit specification gaming rates as high as 47.9%, while no gaming is observed in the evaluated ARG configurations. These results indicate that scaffolding design and integrity checks are important considerations when deploying agents for autonomous ML experimentation.

</details>


### [25] [FleetSieve: Decision-Critical Profiling for SLO-Aware LLM Fleet Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19659)
*Huang Cheng,Scott Zhang,Aubert Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FleetSieve提出了一种针对LLM服务集群的智能测量选择方法，通过联合建模容量和尾部延迟、比较保守与乐观分配，仅在剩余决策间隙低于指定容差时停止测量，从而以显著少于随机完整剖析的GPU时间达到Oracle级别的资源分配决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM服务集群中，选择张量并行（TP）度和副本数量非常困难，因为性能对TP并非单调，且可行配置随负载变化。穷举剖析虽然能消除不确定性，但会测量大量不影响最终资源分配的配置，造成显著的计算浪费。

Method: FleetSieve根据测量对资源耦合、SLO感知集群决策的预期影响来选择测量项。它联合建模容量和尾部延迟，比较保守与乐观两种分配方案，当两者之间的剩余决策间隙低于指定容差时停止测量，从而在不确定性和测量成本之间取得平衡。

Result: 在固定H100测量网格上，针对31B参数开放权重模型，FleetSieve使用22,200 GPU秒达到Oracle聚合决策，比均匀随机剖析节省6.9%的GPU时间；在200个随机揭示顺序中，平均节省5.4%（95% bootstrap CI: 3.5-7.2%）。Chat场景固定比较节省21.5%，Code场景未使用最少的GPU秒。联合容量和尾部建模避免选择了46.4秒p99违反30秒SLO的配置。在16-GPU分配中，错误的稀疏剖析决策损失最多1.93请求/秒和12.4个百分点的max-min满足率。

Conclusion: FleetSieve通过按预期影响采样测量、联合建模容量与尾部延迟，有效消除了无关测量开销，同时避免了SLO违规和资源分配损失。边界重复和BurstGPT测量验证了观测到的负载依赖尾部延迟机制的真实性。

Abstract: Choosing tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and replica counts for an LLM serving fleet is difficult because performance is not monotonic in TP and the feasible choice can change with load. Exhaustive profiling resolves this uncertainty, but measures many configurations that do not affect the final resource allocation. We present FleetSieve, which selects measurements according to their expected effect on a resource-coupled, SLO-aware fleet decision. FleetSieve models capacity and tail latency jointly, compares conservative and optimistic allocations, and stops when their remaining decision gap is below a specified tolerance. On a fixed H100 measurement grid for a 31B-parameter open-weight model, FleetSieve reaches the oracle aggregate decision using 22,200 GPU-seconds, 6.9% less than uniform random profiling in the fixed comparison. Across 200 random reveal orders, its mean saving over random profiling is 5.4% (95% bootstrap CI: 3.5-7.2%). The fixed-comparison saving is 21.5% for Chat, while FleetSieve does not use the fewest GPU-seconds for Code. Joint capacity and tail modeling also avoids selecting a configuration whose 46.4-second completion p99 violates a 30-second SLO. In a 16-GPU allocation, an incorrect sparse-profile decision loses up to 1.93 requests/s and 12.4 percentage points of max-min fulfillment. Boundary repeats and BurstGPT measurements support the observed load-dependent tail-latency mechanism.

</details>


### [26] [SAGE-XGBoost: Spatially Augmented Graph Embeddings--Machine Learning Framework for Natural Hazards Susceptibility Mapping under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19672)
*Mohammad H. Vahidnia,Ali Pourkarimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（空间增强图嵌入）框架，通过受控噪声数据增强与基于K近邻图的图嵌入结合，在标注数据稀缺条件下提升滑坡和野火易发性预测性能。SAGE-XGBoost模型在两个案例中均优于传统机器学习和空间显式机器学习模型，AUC分别达约0.97（滑坡）和0.95（野火）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害易发性制图常受限于标注数据不足，导致传统机器学习泛化能力下降，且复杂深度学习模型在此条件下难以应用。因此需要一个高效且可迁移的特征工程框架，在有限监督下提升预测性能。

Method: SAGE框架结合两类特征增强策略：(1) 受控噪声数据增强，增加训练数据的多样性；(2) K近邻图构建邻域空间统计量，经主成分分析降维后与环境协变量及空间坐标融合。所得特征输入XGBoost构成SAGE-XGBoost模型，并在滑坡和野火易发性制图中进行验证。

Result: SAGE-XGBoost在两个案例中一致优于传统及空间显式机器学习模型。与Spatial XGBoost相比，绝对改进超33个百分点；滑坡易发性AUC约0.97，野火易发性AUC约0.95。特征重要性分析证实图嵌入的贡献，且其集成提升了空间连贯性并降低了局部噪声放大。

Conclusion: SAGE-XGBoost为数据稀缺条件下环境灾害评估及其他地理空间预测任务提供了一种高效、可迁移的深度学习表示学习替代方案。

Abstract: Natural hazard susceptibility mapping is often constrained by limited labeled data, reducing the generalizability of conventional machine learning and limiting the applicability of complex deep learning models. This study proposes SAGE (Spatially Augmented Graph Embeddings), a structurally informed feature-engineering framework that combines controlled noise-based data augmentation with neighborhood-based graph embeddings to improve prediction under data-scarce conditions. A K-nearest neighbor graph is constructed to derive local spatial statistics, which are reduced using principal component analysis and integrated with environmental covariates and spatial coordinates. The resulting features are used with XGBoost to develop the SAGE-XGBoost model. The framework was evaluated for landslide and wildfire susceptibility mapping. SAGE-XGBoost consistently outperformed conventional and spatially explicit machine learning models. Compared with Spatial XGBoost, it achieved an absolute improvement of above 33 percentage points across the two case studies. The model reached AUC values of approximately 0.97 for landslide susceptibility and 0.95 for wildfire susceptibility. Feature importance analysis confirmed the contribution of graph embeddings to prediction, while their integration improved spatial coherence and reduced local noise amplification. Overall, SAGE-XGBoost provides an efficient and transferable alternative to deep representation learning for environmental hazard assessment and other geospatial prediction tasks under limited supervision.

</details>


### [27] [A Locally Tokenized Generative Model for Robust Time-Series Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19727)
*Dongbin Kim,Geonwoo Shin,Yujin Choi,Soyeon Park,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多变量时间序列生成模型中的水印可靠性问题，提出L-VQVAE和LVQMark方法，通过局部邻域依赖的离散令牌生成与鲁棒重编码，克服了后编辑攻击下检测器的双向漂移问题，在金融、能源和神经影像四个基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印检测器依赖全局耦合的重编码机制，在后编辑攻击下会导致非水印样本的z-score发生双向漂移，从而使得基于干净数据校准的阈值失效。作者认为这种不稳定性源于重编码的全局耦合特性，需要使每个恢复单元仅依赖有界的时序邻域才能实现可靠检测。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) L-VQVAE，一种生成模型，其中每个离散令牌由短连续窗口生成，确保局部依赖；(2) LVQMark，基于该令牌空间的水印方法，结合logit-bias注入和鲁棒重编码用于攻击时检测。

Result: 在金融、能源和神经影像四个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持生成质量的同时，在后编辑攻击下稳定了检测能力和假阳性行为。

Conclusion: 局部时序邻域依赖是解决生成模型水印在后编辑攻击下可靠的检测的关键原则，L-VQVAE和LVQMark通过该原则实现了稳定且可靠的检测性能。

Abstract: Watermarking is a central tool for provenance in generative models, yet its application to multivariate time series remains hindered by reliability failures under post-editing attacks. We show that existing detectors, which rely on globally coupled re-encoding, suffer from bidirectional drift of the null distribution: post-editing attacks can shift the z-score of non-watermarked samples in either direction, invalidating clean-calibrated thresholds. We argue that this instability is a property of the re-encoding, and that reliable detection requires each recovered unit to depend only on a bounded temporal neighborhood. Guided by this principle, we propose L-VQVAE, a generative model in which each discrete token is produced from a short contiguous window, and LVQMark, a watermarking method over this token space that combines logit-bias injection with robust re-encoding for attack-time detection. Experiments on four benchmarks spanning finance, energy, and neuroimaging show that our approach preserves generation quality while stabilizing both detection power and false-positive behavior under post-editing attacks.

</details>


### [28] [RecPFN: Prior-Fitted Networks for In-Context-Based Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19735)
*En Zhi Tan,Jia Xiang Lim,Bryan Lijie Chew,Tze Minh Ng,Benjamin Yan Han Yap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecPFN是一个基于先验拟合网络的顺序推荐方法，通过将上下文学习引入推荐系统，完全在合成点击流环境中预训练，实现从少量支持集中隐式执行贝叶斯推理，无需权重更新即可在单次前向传播中完成预测。在8个公开基准上达到最先进的零样本性能，在低计算和低数据场景下与监督方法竞争，且部署高效、对域迁移鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列推荐模型依赖于大规模真实交互数据，在数据稀缺或域迁移场景下泛化能力有限。作者旨在探索一条通用且数据高效的推荐路径，通过将上下文学习（in-context learning）与贝叶斯风格推理引入推荐系统，使模型能从极少量领域数据中快速适应，而无需重新训练。

Method: RecPFN采用先验拟合网络（Prior-Fitted Network）架构，包含两部分：(1) 预训练阶段：在从广泛结构因果先验中采样的纯合成点击流环境上训练一个轻量级decoder-only transformer，使其学会摊销贝叶斯推理；(2) 推理阶段：模型以少量域序列作为条件，对查询执行单次前向传播即可生成下一项预测，无需任何权重更新。

Result: 在8个公开基准数据集上，RecPFN取得了最先进的零样本性能，在低计算和低数据场景下与监督方法保持强竞争力。相比依赖大规模真实交互语料的强零样本基线，其在域迁移场景下表现更优，且部署效率更高。

Conclusion: RecPFN为构建通用、数据高效的推荐系统提供了可行路径，表明合成数据上的先验拟合训练能够替代大规模真实数据的依赖。未来工作可探索更丰富的先验、更长上下文的ICL以及多模态扩展。代码已公开。

Abstract: We introduce RecPFN, a prior-fitted network that brings in-context learning to sequential recommendation. RecPFN is pretrained entirely on synthetic clickstream environments sampled from a broad structural causal prior, enabling it to amortize Bayesian-style inference from a small support set. At inference, a lightweight decoder-only transformer conditions on a handful of domain sequences and produces next-item predictions for queries in a single forward pass, without any weight updates. Across eight public benchmarks, RecPFN achives state-of-the-art zero-shot performance while remaining strongly competitive with supervised methods in low-compute and low-data regimes. It is deployment-efficient and robust to domain shift, outperforming strong zero-shot baselines that rely on large real-interaction corpora. RecPFN provides a practical path toward generalizable, data-efficient recommenders and opens avenues for richer priors, longer-context ICL, and multimodal extensions. Code for training and evaluation is publicly available at https://github.com/SAP-samples/tabular-ai-recpfn/.

</details>


### [29] [Truncate Bad, Upweight Good: BoN-Style Distillation via Rank-Based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19748)
*Yarin Bar,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TUP（Truncate-bad, Upweight-good Policy），一种通过截断低排名样本并仅对保留的高排名尾部进行可调锐度的重加权来蒸馏Best-of-N等推理时选择方法的策略。TUP支持闭式、与提示无关的归一化，可通过二元交叉熵全离线训练，并在理论上证明截断低尾部优于单纯降权，经验上与强离线对齐基线竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于排名的蒸馏策略通常采用平滑的全支撑重加权，导致低排名完成结果虽被降权但仍在目标支持中。虽然更尖锐的重加权可以减少低尾部概率质量，但也会增加对单一奖励模型在顶部做出的脆弱排名的高度依赖。因此需要一种既能锐化分布又不过度依赖奖励模型排名可靠性的方法。

Method: TUP方法：（1）从支撑集中去除低排名完成结果（截断bad尾部）；（2）仅对保留的高排名尾部使用可调锐度参数进行重加权（提升good尾部）；（3）支持闭式、提示无关的归一化；（4）使用移位截断胜率（shifted-truncated win-rates）作为软标签、蒸馏-参考对数似然比作为logits，通过二元交叉熵完全离线训练；（5）理论上证明在特定假设下，任何最优单调排名重加权都可以被低尾部截断规则匹配。

Result: 理论上证明了对于任何未知的oracle奖励，最佳单调排名重加权可以由低尾部截断规则实现，为截断低尾部而非简单降权提供了正式支持。经验上，TUP与强离线对齐基线（如DPO类方法）具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 截断低排名完成结果比单纯降权更有效，TUP提供了一种理论上可证明、计算高效且全离线可训练的蒸馏方案，在不牺牲性能的前提下简化了对推理时采样选择的蒸馏过程，并减少对单一奖励模型顶部排名脆弱性的依赖。

Abstract: Inference-time selection methods, such as Best-of-N, improve generation by sampling a pool of candidates and selecting the top-ranked completion according to a reward model. Distillation seeks to amortize this procedure into a single policy by replacing raw rewards with in-pool ranks and learning a policy that upweights higher-ranked completions. However, existing rank-based policies typically use smooth full-support reweighting, so low-ranked completions receive less mass but remain in the target support. Although a sharper reweighting reduces lower-tail mass, it also increases reliance on brittle ranking at the top made by a single reward model. We propose TUP: a Truncate-bad, Upweight-good Policy that removes low-ranked completions from the support and reweights only the retained upper tail with a tunable sharpness. TUP admits a closed-form, prompt-independent normalization and can be trained fully offline via binary cross-entropy, using shifted-truncated win-rates as soft labels and distilled-to-reference log-likelihood ratios as logits. Theoretically, under certain assumptions, we show that for any unknown oracle reward, the best monotone rank-reweighting can be matched by a lower-tail truncation rule, providing formal support for removing the lower tail rather than merely downweighting it. Empirically, we show that TUP is competitive with strong offline alignment baselines.

</details>


### [30] [Credit Without Ground Truth: Auditing Step-Level Credit Assignment in LLM Agents Against Executed Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19760)
*Haiyue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在单智能体工具环境（ALFWorld）中，基于实际重放的因果真值审计了LLM智能体训练中使用的各类步骤级信用信号（LLM裁判分数、结果条件对数概率比、策略自身置信度），发现所有这些信号在识别因果关键步骤上的表现都不优于随机水平。现有评估以标注的步骤"正确性"为标准，本文则以步骤"贡献"（重采样策略备选并前向推演对结果的实际影响）为标准进行审计，两者存在显著背离。因果贡献具有稀疏性（30.5%的决策点有可测效应）且可测性依赖模型（无可支持反事实的点比例在相似规模策略间相差两倍）。失败模式可识别：隐含信用反映策略流畅度（中位数秩相关+0.75），而结果条件化不增加因果信息（偏相关-0.004）。仅用置信度的路由在恢复关键步骤上仍为随机水平，但每回合可节省13.1%的裁判成本。七臂预注册训练实验中无一臂稳定超越未训练策略，且检查点出现的工具信号完全由训练剂量（有效样本量或优化器步数）解释，而非信用内容本身。因此，信用规则的比较必须匹配有效样本量，否则测量的只是剂量而非信用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体训练中的步骤级信用信号（LLM裁判评分、结果条件对数概率比、策略置信度）虽然被广泛使用，但其评估一直基于标注的步骤"正确性"而非实际的因果"贡献"。本文旨在更正这一评估谬误，用因果重放真值来审计这些信用信号是否真的能识别对最终结果有因果影响的关键步骤，并揭示其失败模式及评估中的混杂因素。

Method: 在ALFWorld单智能体工具环境中执行重放实验，以重采样策略自身各决策点的备选动作并前向推演是否改变结果来定义步骤因果贡献的ground truth。计算三类信用信号（LLM裁判分数、结果条件对数概率比、策略置信度）与因果贡献的关联，并与"正确性"标注下的评估进行对照。进一步进行七臂预注册训练实验，比较不同信用规则训练出的检查点性能，并利用仪器变量及有效样本量控制来分析混淆变量。

Result: 所有三类信用信号识别因果关键步骤的能力均不优于随机水平。因果贡献稀疏（30.5%的决策点有可测效应），且可测性依赖模型（无可支持反事实的点比例在两种策略间为13.1% vs 26.8%）。隐含信用与策略流畅度高度相关（中位秩相关+0.75，第二家族在修正仪器下复现+0.70），结果条件化不提供因果信息（偏相关-0.004）。置信度路由可在关键步骤恢复上保持随机水平的同时每回合节省13.1%裁判成本。七臂训练实验中无臂超越未训练策略，检查点的仪器信号被训练剂量（有效样本量、优化器步数相差一个数量级）完全解释。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体使用的步骤级信用信号未能有效识别因果关键步骤，其表面上的有效性源于评估标准的偏差（以正确性代替贡献）。信用规则比较实验必须匹配有效样本量，否则比较的是训练剂量而非信用内容本身；因果贡献的结构特性（稀疏性、模型依赖性）也为未来设计更好的信用分配机制提供了依据。

Abstract: Audited against causal ground truth from executed replay in a single-agent tool environment (ALFWorld), none of the step-level credit signals used to train LLM agents -- LLM-judge scores, outcome-conditioned logprob ratios, or the policy's own confidence -- identifies which steps causally matter better than chance. Existing evaluations grade these signals against annotated step *correctness*; we audit them against step *contribution* -- what re-sampling the policy's own alternatives at each decision point and rolling forward actually changes about the outcome -- and the two come apart. The ground truth itself is structured: causal contribution is sparse (30.5% of decision points where ground truth is defined carry measurable effect), and measurability is model-dependent -- the fraction of points with no policy-supported counterfactual differs by a factor of two (13.1% vs. 26.8%) between two similar-scale policies. The failure mode is identifiable: implicit credit echoes the policy's fluency (median rank correlation +0.75, replicating at +0.70 in a second family under a corrected instrument), while conditioning on the outcome adds no causal information (partial correlation -0.004, Qwen). A confidence-only router recovers pivotal steps at chance level, but cuts judge cost by 13.1% per turn (14.0% per trajectory). In a seven-arm pre-registered training experiment, no arm reliably outperforms the untrained policy, and the checkpoints' apparent instrument signature is fully explained by training dose -- sparser credit retains fewer examples, an order-of-magnitude spread in optimizer steps -- not credit content. Comparisons of credit rules must therefore match effective sample size, or they measure dose, not credit.

</details>


### [31] [Finite-Horizon Input-Output Dynamics of Minibatch Perturbations in AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19762)
*Kang Liu,Suyan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdamW优化器中minibatch对训练的延迟影响。通过配对轨迹（仅在一个梯度更新处不同、后续训练序列相同）将AdamW建模为有限时域输入-状态-输出(ISO)系统，利用线性化推导出带符号的响应算子来刻画梯度扰动对未来损失的影响。文章还推导了精确的多步误差分解，并在局部光滑性条件下建立了有限时域的一阶精度保证。实验验证了响应机制和优化器状态效应，发现延迟影响中具有显著的前瞻性结构，可通过ISO近似部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因为AdamW的优化器状态（一阶矩和二阶矩估计）存储了过去的梯度信息，所以一个minibatch的影响会超越其被观察时的那个更新步骤，对后续训练产生延迟效应。理解这种延迟影响的机制对于理解AdamW的训练动态和可解释性至关重要。

Method: 将AdamW形式化为一个有限时域的输入-状态-输出(ISO)系统，系统的状态包含模型参数、一阶矩估计和二阶矩估计。通过对联合动态进行线性化，推导出一个带符号的响应算子，将局部梯度扰动映射到其未来的损失影响上。此外，推导了精确的多步误差分解，并在局部光滑性和受控激活切换条件下建立了有限时域的一阶精度保证。实验部分通过配对轨迹验证了响应机制，并利用重复未来分析揭示了延迟影响的前瞻性结构。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的响应机制和优化器状态的延迟效应。重复未来分析表明，延迟影响中存在着显著的前瞻性结构，这些结构可以通过ISO线性近似部分恢复。代码已开源。

Conclusion: AdamW优化器状态会对训练产生可预测的延迟影响，通过ISO系统框架可以有效刻画和近似这种延迟效应，为理解优化器记忆机制提供了新的理论分析工具。

Abstract: A minibatch can influence training beyond the update at which it is observed because AdamW stores past gradient information in its optimizer states. We study this delayed effect through paired trajectories that differ only in one gradient update and share the same subsequent training sequence. We formulate AdamW as a finite-horizon input--state--output (ISO) system whose state contains the model parameters and first- and second-moment estimates. Linearizing the joint dynamics yields a signed response operator that maps a localized gradient perturbation to its future loss effects, revealing how optimizer memory shapes their magnitude, timing, and sign. We further derive an exact multistep error decomposition and establish first-order finite-horizon accuracy under local smoothness and controlled activation switching. Experiments validate the response mechanism and optimizer-state effects, while repeated-future analyses reveal substantial prospective structure in delayed influence that can be partially recovered from ISO approximations. Code is available at https://github.com/Kanyooo/Loss_ISO.

</details>


### [32] [Unsupervised Anomaly Detection Using Flow Matching on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19801)
*Philip Konz,Tejaswini Medi,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了流匹配（flow matching）方法在训练数据受污染情况下的无监督表格异常检测表现，发现异常评分函数的选择比生成模型本身更关键，轨迹类评分（Deviation和Reconstruction）比TCCM原始的Decision评分对污染更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融异常检测常依赖大规模未标注交易日志，训练集中可能已经包含异常样本，违背了多数异常检测方法的"干净正常数据"假设。流匹配在生成建模中表现优异，但其在无监督表格异常检测中对污染训练数据的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对比了Time-Conditioned Contraction Matching (TCCM)与Forest-Flow两种流匹配方法在受污染训练数据下的异常检测性能，并评估了多种异常评分函数（包括原始单步Decision评分、轨迹类Deviation和Reconstruction评分）。在严重类别不平衡的金融数据上进行实验。

Result: 异常评分函数的选择至关重要：TCCM原始的Decision评分对训练集污染敏感，而Deviation和Reconstruction等轨迹类评分提供了更稳定的异常信号。采用这些评分后，Forest-Flow与TCCM相当甚至在一些情况下更优。

Conclusion: 流匹配方法在金融异常检测中的效果很大程度上取决于异常评分函数的选择，而不仅仅是生成模型的架构。轨迹类评分对训练集污染具有更强的鲁棒性，凸显了在严重类别不平衡和标签污染场景下设计异常评分的重要性。

Abstract: Financial anomaly detection often relies on large unlabeled transaction logs, where anomalous samples may already be present during training. Such training-set contamination violates the clean-normal data assumption underlying many anomaly detection methods. Although flow matching has demonstrated strong performance in generative modeling, its robustness in unsupervised tabular anomaly detection remains underexplored. In this work, we study flow-matching-based anomaly detection under contaminated training data by comparing Time-Conditioned Contraction Matching (TCCM) with Forest-Flow and evaluating multiple anomaly scoring functions. Our results show that the choice of anomaly score is critical. The original single-step Decision score used by TCCM is sensitive to contamination, whereas trajectory-based Deviation and Reconstruction scores provide more stable anomaly signals. With these scores, Forest-Flow becomes competitive with, and in some cases outperforms, TCCM. These findings highlight the importance of anomaly scoring for flow-matching methods in financial anomaly detection under severe class imbalance.

</details>


### [33] [MileGPO: Milestone Inference with Local Evidence for Graph-Based Policy Optimization of Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19803)
*Bo Qian,Yuting Wu,Shuang Zeng,Huaiyu Wan,Dalin Zhang,Jiqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MileGPO框架，通过里程碑发现、可靠性校准整形和进度对比校准三个设计，从分组同策略rollout中推导过程级信用分配，无需辅助模型或额外环境交互，在ALFWorld和WebShop上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体强化学习中信用分配具有挑战性，监督信号往往仅来自最终奖励。现有方法通过步骤分组或基于图的优势估计将轨迹级信号细化为步骤级信用，但可能忽视有意义的中间里程碑，导致信用分配不够精确。

Method: MileGPO包含三个核心设计：(1) 里程碑发现：在成功rollout中识别候选里程碑，在失败rollout中识别重复出现的陷阱；(2) 可靠性校准整形（RCS）：基于结果置信度对这些候选进行加权，强化可靠里程碑和陷阱，降低不确定候选的权重；(3) 进度对比校准（PCC）：检验候选是否反映局部进度，以及其传入转换是否优于相同状态下的观测替代方案。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上取得SOTA性能，且在ALFWorld上分布内到分布外的性能差距较小。消融实验和信用诊断表明，可靠性加权、局部进度和同状态分支证据能互补里程碑发现，并解决模糊的中间信用分配问题。

Conclusion: MileGPO通过从分组rollout中提取过程级信用，有效解决了长时程智能体强化学习中的中间里程碑信用分配问题，无需辅助模型或额外环境交互即可提升性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Credit assignment is challenging in long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning, where supervision often comes only from final rewards. Existing methods refine trajectory-level signals into step-level credits through step grouping or graph-based advantage estimation, but can overlook meaningful intermediate milestones. We propose MileGPO (Milestone Inference with Local Evidence for Graph-Based Policy Optimization), which derives process-level credit from grouped on-policy rollouts through three designs. Milestone Discovery identifies candidate milestones on successful rollouts and recurring traps on failed ones. Reliability-Calibrated Shaping (RCS) weights these candidates by outcome-based confidence, strengthening reliable milestones and traps while down-weighting uncertain ones. Progress-Contrastive Calibration (PCC) further tests whether a candidate reflects local progress and whether its incoming ansition outperforms observed alternatives from the same state.MileGPO requires neither auxiliary models nor additional environment interaction. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show state-of-the-art performance and a small in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap on ALFWorld. Ablations and credit diagnostics indicate that reliability weighting, local progress, and same-state branch evidence complement milestone discovery and resolve ambiguous intermediate credit.

</details>


### [34] [Answer-Level Trust Selection for Physical Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19807)
*Rongyu Yu,Ke Niu,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Answer-Level Trust Selection (ATS)，一个无需微调、无需辅助验证器、无需内部logits的模型无关框架，用于对VLM在定量物理推理中的单个预测进行接受或拒绝的判断。ATS聚合八个可解释的行为诊断分数（基于重复查询和受控干预），识别出仅靠自一致性无法捕获的稳定但错误（stable-but-wrong）和依赖文本先验而非视觉证据的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM物理量估计基准主要评估整体模型性能，但在部署场景中，单个预测的可信度判断至关重要——因为真实标签通常不可得。仅依赖自一致性（self-consistency）无法捕获关键失败模式，例如VLM可能产生稳定但错误的估计，或依赖文本先验而非视觉证据。因此需要一个能够在答案层面评估单个预测可信度的框架。

Method: ATS是一个post-hoc、模型无关的框架。它不要求微调、辅助验证器或内部logits访问，而是通过重复查询和受控干预聚合八个可解释的行为诊断分数，统一为一个信任分数（trust score），用于接受或拒绝单个VLM预测。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上进行深度评估，并在20个VLM骨干网络上进行广泛评估。结果显示：基于干预的诊断方法有助于识别仅靠重复一致性可能遗漏的stable-but-wrong和prior-tracking预测，但改进失败案例拒绝可能以降低正确预测保留率为代价。

Conclusion: ATS通过答案层面的可靠性评估补充了模型层面的能力评估，为定量VLM预测提供了可选择性的信任判断机制，有助于部署场景中对单个预测的可信度进行决策。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can estimate physical quantities such as duration, speed, and acceleration from visual observations, but existing benchmarks primarily assess overall model performance against annotated ground truth. In deployment, a key question is whether an individual prediction can be trusted when its ground truth is unavailable. Self-consistency alone may fail to capture important failure modes: a VLM may produce stable-but-wrong estimates or rely on textual priors rather than visual evidence. We formulate answer-level selective prediction for quantitative physical reasoning and propose Answer-Level Trust Selection (ATS), a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework for accepting or rejecting individual VLM predictions. ATS requires no fine-tuning, auxiliary verifier, or access to the model's internal logits. Instead, it aggregates eight interpretable behavioral diagnostic scores derived from repeated queries and controlled interventions into a unified trust score. We evaluate ATS in depth on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and across 20 VLM backbones, examining selective performance, diagnostic behavior, and targeted failure modes. Our results show that intervention-based diagnostics help identify stable-but-wrong and prior-tracking predictions that repeated agreement alone may miss. However, improved failure-case rejection can come at the cost of lower retention of correct predictions. ATS therefore complements model-level capability evaluation with answer-level reliability assessment for quantitative VLM predictions. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>

*Guofeng Zhang,Rong Han,Xiaoyu Wang,Zhiyun Li,Zongbo Han,Xiaohong Liu,Guangyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG
