### [66] [The Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP): A Portable Contract for AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19263)
*Aniket Wattamwar,Manav Anandani,Mrunal Kakirwar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出评估上下文协议（ECP），一个早期、供应商中立的框架，作为智能体系统的可移植评估契约层，通过JSON-RPC接口暴露用户可见输出、工具调用和审计上下文，支持跨框架和CI系统的统一程序化检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI从孤立的大语言模型评估转向自主智能体架构评估，但现有评估范式存在严重缺陷，包括基准测试钻漏洞、"自信地错误"现象、理论能力与实际可靠性之间的差距，以及评估基础设施碎片化问题。

Method: 提出ECP协议，定义了一个小型JSON-RPC接口，智能体通过该接口暴露其用户可见输出、工具调用和评估者安全的审计上下文，程序化检查可跨框架和持续集成系统统一运行。提供开源参考实现，包含LangChain、LlamaIndex、CrewAI和PydanticAI的适配器。

Result: 该论文描述了ECP的开源参考实现及四个智能体框架的适配器，并针对近期文献中记载的失败模式对设计进行了定位。然而，ECP被明确视为进行中的工作而非最终标准，评估面、方法集和评分器家族预计会随协议在更多系统上应用而变化。

Conclusion: ECP作为早期原型旨在缓解当前评估基础设施的碎片化，但其经验验证和采用论证被列为未来工作，表明该协议尚需在更多真实系统和场景中得到验证和迭代。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence has necessitated a fundamental shift from evaluating isolated Large Language Models (LLMs) to assessing autonomous agentic architectures. This paper explores the critical methodologies for evaluating AI agents and the essential role of advanced observability infrastructure. We analyze the architectural components of agents and identify the severe limitations of current evaluation paradigms, including benchmark exploitation, the "confidently wrong" phenomenon, and the discrepancy between theoretical capability and operational reliability. To begin addressing the fragmentation in current evaluation infrastructure, this paper proposes the Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP), an early-stage, vendor-neutral framework intended to act as a portable evaluation contract layer for agentic systems. In its current form ECP defines a small JSON-RPC interface over which an agent exposes its user-visible output, the tool calls it made, and evaluator-safe audit context, and against which programmatic checks can be run uniformly across frameworks and continuous integration systems. We describe an open-source reference implementation that includes adapters for LangChain, LlamaIndex, CrewAI, and PydanticAI, and we situate the design against failure modes documented in the recent literature. ECP is presented as work in progress rather than a finished standard: the evaluation surface, method set, and grader families are all expected to change as the protocol is exercised against more systems, and the empirical validation required to justify adoption is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [67] [Stable Within, Unidentified Across: Semantic Identification of Benchmark Effects and Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19269)
*Xi Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估语义标识"概念，通过一族受控的评估端点（evaluation-semantic endpoints）考察评估结论的稳定性。基于217行冻结数据的研究表明，结论在限定契约族内稳定，但不同对比族（F_cc vs F_asym vs F_audit）对目标参数T的标识结果不同，差异100%归因于单侧终端删除，呈现局部非蕴含证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估结论在很大程度上取决于评估者所控制的语义选择，包括法律引用（legal references）、可评分性（scoreability）和聚合方式（aggregation）。作者旨在揭示这些语义选择如何影响结论的识别性，从而为评估指标提供可复用的族索引审计框架，而非仅仅给出单一结论。

Method: 本文提出一个新的分析概念——"评估语义标识"（evaluation-semantically identified, EVSI）。当一个由工件定义的端点Q(s)在声明的家族上保持不变时，称其为评估语义标识的。方法上采用冻结的217行数据集，在三个受限对比家族（F_asym、F_cc及其并集F_audit）中进行任务分离的采纳效应估计，并采用因子审计（factorial audit）将族间分歧分解为非终结与终结贡献。

Result: F_asym在任务分离的采纳效应上产生从-40.76到-43.03个百分点范围内的精确估计；F_cc中两个联合冻结的可比对比处理在[0,0]区间上恰好为零；两者的并集F_audit无法标识T，尽管F_cc能标识T=0。因子审计表明100%的分歧归因于单侧终端删除，非终结贡献为零。排序在不同对称处理间仍依赖具体规格。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个局部非蕴含反例（non-implication witness）和可复用的族索引审计，而非流行率估计、穷举语义分类或独立复现。其核心启示是：评估结论的识别性高度依赖所选的评估语义家族，指标设计者需明确声明的族范围并警惕边界选择带来的结论翻转。

Abstract: Evaluation conclusions depend on evaluator-controlled semantics: legal references, scoreability, and aggregation. We call an artifact-defined endpoint Q(s) evaluation-semantically identified when it is invariant over a declared family. A frozen 217-row analysis appears stable within its restricted contract family. In TraceElephant, F_asym yields precise task-disjoint adoption effects from -40.76 to -43.03 percentage points, whereas both jointly frozen contrast-comparable treatments in F_cc yield exactly zero with [0, 0] intervals. Their union F_audit therefore does not identify T, even though F_cc identifies T = 0. A factorial audit attributes 100 percent of the disagreement to one-sided terminal deletion, with zero nonterminal contribution. Rankings remain specification-dependent across the symmetric treatments. This is a localized non-implication witness and reusable family-indexed audit, not a prevalence estimate, exhaustive semantic taxonomy, or independent replication.

</details>


### [68] [Hype Meets Reality: Large Language Models as Mutators in Search-based Automated Program Repair of Simulink-Stateflow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19347)
*Ayesha Irshad,Pablo Valle,Jon Ayerdi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在Cyper-Physical Systems（CPS）的Simulink/Stateflow模型中，将LLM生成的突变算子集成到基于搜索的自动程序修复（APR）框架FlowRepair中，以替代部分手动设计的突变算子。在19个真实故障模型基准上的受控实验表明，LLM突变大幅降低了修复性能（可修复模型从18/16降至4-6/4），原因是LLM难以进行精确符号编辑、缺乏行为反馈、且生成了嘈杂的搜索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于搜索的APR依赖精心设计的手动突变算子来探索候选修复空间，而LLM具有动态生成修复建议的潜力，可能替代这些算子以提高修复的表达力和灵活性。作者希望验证这一假设在CPS的Simulink/Stateflow建模场景中是否成立。

Method: 扩展了最先进的FlowRepair方法，将其中一部分突变算子替换为LLM生成的突变，在保持与FlowRepair相同的实验设置（包括相同的wall-clock预算）条件下进行受控对比评估，测试多种LLM变体。

Result: 在19个真实故障Stateflow模型（覆盖4个CPS领域）的基准上，LLM-based修复仅为4-6个模型生成plausible补丁、4个模型生成valid补丁，而原始FlowRepair分别为18和16个模型。LLM突变显著降低了修复性能。

Conclusion: LLM在集成到搜索式APR时，无法进行精确的符号级编辑、缺乏行为反馈，且生成嘈杂的搜索空间阻碍有效探索。这并非表明LLM对APR的普遍局限，而是揭示了简单地将LLM嵌入搜索式APR的根本性缺陷，未来应探索将结构化突变与生成式引导相结合的混合方法。

Abstract: Search-based Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques rely on carefully designed mutation operators to explore the space of candidate fixes. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that generative models could replace such operators by dynamically proposing repairs. In this paper, we investigate this hypothesis in the context of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) modeled in Simulink/Stateflow. We extend the state-of-the-art FlowRepair approach by replacing a subset of its mutation operators with LLM-generated mutations, enabling more flexible and expressive patch generation. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark of 19 real-world faulty Stateflow models across four CPS domains, using the same experimental setup as FlowRepair for controlled comparison under the same wall-clock budget. Contrary to expectations, in this controlled evaluation, the LLM-based mutation substantially degrades repair performance under the FlowRepair experimental setup. Across the tested LLM variants, the LLM-based repair produced plausible patches for 4-6 models and valid patches for 4 models, compared to 18 and 16, respectively, with the original approach. Our analysis reveals that, in this integration, LLMs struggle with precise symbolic edits, lack behavioral feedback, and generate a noisy search space that hinders effective exploration. Rather than showing a general limitation of LLMs for APR, these findings highlight fundamental limitations of naively integrating LLMs into search-based APR and motivate hybrid approaches that combine structured mutation with generative guidance.

</details>


### [69] [RD-Gen: Random DAG Generator Considering Multi-rate Applications for Reproducible Scheduling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19460)
*Atsushi Yano,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RD-Gen，一个面向多速率实时应用的随机有向无环图（DAG）生成工具，支持批量生成不同参数的随机DAG集，用于可复现的调度评估，解决了缺乏考虑多速率应用的随机DAG生成工具的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统日益庞大复杂，DAG模型广泛用于任务依赖性和并行性的表达，随机DAG集用于验证方法的有效性与客观性。然而现有工具无法生成考虑最新多速率应用的随机DAG集，研究者需自行生成，导致额外工作量、可靠性降低且难以复现。

Method: 提出RD-Gen，一种考虑多速率应用的随机DAG生成器，用于可复现的调度评估。该工具支持批量生成具有不同参数的随机DAG集，并通过案例研究验证其能管理各种问题设置和DAG研究需求。

Result: 通过案例研究证明了RD-Gen能够处理各种问题设置，满足DAG研究的需求，可生成考虑多速率应用的随机DAG集并支持批量参数化生成。

Conclusion: RD-Gen填补了多速率随机DAG生成工具的空白，提升了DAG调度评估的可靠性、可复现性和研究效率。

Abstract: Real-time systems have various requirements such as the deadline and resource constraints. In addition, real-time systems are becoming larger and more complex, and studies on performance analysis and efficient scheduling algorithms are becoming increasingly important. Directed acyclic graph (DAG) models, which can express task dependencies and parallelism, are used for such studies. Random DAG sets are used to demonstrate the effectiveness and objectivity of methods proposed for real-time systems. However, there is no random DAG generation tool available that can generate a DAG set that considers the latest multi-rate applications. Therefore, researchers need to generate random DAG sets on their own, leading to additional effort and reduced reliability and reproducibility. To solve this problem, we propose a random DAG generator considering multi-rate applications for reproducible scheduling evaluation (RD-Gen). RD-Gen also enables batch generation of random DAG sets with different parameters. Case studies are used to demonstrate that RD-Gen can manage various problem settings and DAG study requirements.

</details>


### [70] [Measuring What a Specification Determines: A Formal Semantic-Block Model and an Execution-Judged Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19475)
*Oleg Grynets,Dmytro Kostetskyi,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种正式的语义块规范模型（semantic-block model）和一种基于执行判定的基准（execution-judged benchmark），用于独立于模型能力评估规范质量。该模型将规范表示为包含语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和开放问题的结构，并受四个机器可检查的良构条件约束。确定性（determinacy）在模型论层面定义为所有符合实现的共识，并通过独立实施者间的收敛性进行经验估计。在Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移规范上的实证研究未支持将确定性作为独立质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范质量评估常常与模型能力耦合，难以独立衡量规范本身的质量。作者旨在将规范表示形式化，并设计一个不依赖模型能力的基准来评估规范质量，特别是考察"确定性"（determinacy）这一形式概念是否可作为实用的经验质量指标。

Method: 提出五层语义块分解模型，包含18个语义块和19条依赖边的Oracle-to-PostgreSQL迁移规范作为实例。模型满足四个机器可检查的良构条件（无环性、单一所有权、约束支配、整体性或歧义停止）。定义模型论的确定性概念，并通过固定实现者委员会、强制无规范控制组、使用PostgreSQL 16和活Oracle实例作为确定性执行判据的基准进行经验估计。设计了六项研究，包括三项预注册操纵和三项诊断分析。

Result: 五层分解将平均每任务上下文减少约71%（通过依赖闭包），覆盖研究定义的Oracle构造分类法的85.5%且所有缺口已完成三分类处理，不被测试的备选分区Pareto支配，且从引用派生边的第99.9百分位可恢复。25单元子样本上的重复运行显示经验变异性下限，中位arm-delta离散度为14.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 确定性作为形式概念得到支持，但作为评估当代LLM实施者的独立经验质量指标则不成立。该工作为规范质量的正式建模和独立评估提供了一个框架，但经验结果表明确定性不足以单独作为质量指标。

Abstract: This work introduces a formal semantic-block model for specifications and an execution-judged benchmark for evaluating specification quality independently of model capability. A specification is represented as a structure comprising semantic blocks, dependency relations, block-owned rules, decision points, and explicitly open questions, subject to four machine-checkable well-formedness conditions: acyclicity, single ownership, constraint domination, and totality or ambiguity-stop. Determinacy is defined model-theoretically as agreement among all conforming implementations and is estimated empirically through convergence across independent implementers. The model is instantiated on an Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration specification containing 18 blocks and 19 dependency edges. Computational validation shows that the five-layer decomposition reduces mean per-task context by approximately 71% through dependency closures, covers 85.5% of the study-defined Oracle construct taxonomy with all identified gaps triaged, is not Pareto-dominated by the tested alternative partitions, and is recovered at the 99.9th percentile from citation-derived edges not used to define the original structure. The benchmark keeps the implementer panel fixed, includes a mandatory no-specification control arm, and uses PostgreSQL 16 and a live Oracle instance as deterministic execution judges. Six designed studies, including three pre-registered manipulations and three diagnostic analyses, further examine specification effects. Repeated runs on a 25-unit subsample reveal an empirical variability floor with a median arm-delta spread of 14.4 percentage points. The results support determinacy as a formal concept but not as a standalone empirical quality metric for the evaluated contemporary LLM implementers.

</details>

*Heyang Thomas Li,Alexander Pletzer,Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用Claude智能体AI对Python离散事件项目调度仿真代码进行系统性重构的方法，将测试运行时间从1,298秒降至200秒以下，每年节省四百万核心小时（约32万新西兰元）的计算资源，且不改变输出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python在科研中广泛使用但定制化代码在大规模实验下性能下降严重，尤其在离散事件项目调度仿真中，顺序状态更新、嵌套循环和面向对象结构限制了编译数值库和GPU加速库的优势。传统优化需要反复进行性能剖析、重构、测试和验证，而研究人员往往缺乏时间或专业软件工程经验进行底层优化。

Method: 采用Claude智能体AI在HPC环境中对真实项目调度工作负载进行系统性重构，具体包括：以代表性基准测试和正确性检查为指导，智能体自动识别性能瓶颈、实施有针对性的优化、评估优化效果，同时研究人员保留最终控制权。

Result: 测试运行时间从1,298秒（约21.6分钟）降低到200秒以下，性能提升约6.5倍以上，且不改变输出结果；每年节省四百万核心小时，折合约32万新西兰元。

Conclusion: 智能体AI驱动的代码重构方法能够有效解决Python离散事件仿真中的性能瓶颈问题，在保证结果正确性的前提下显著提升运行效率并大幅节省计算资源成本，为科研人员提供了一种低成本、高效益的代码优化途径。

Abstract: Python is widely used in scientific research because it enables rapid development and provides rich ecosystems for data analysis, artificial intelligence (AI), and machine learning. However, customized research code can become prohibitively slow as experiments scale. This challenge is particularly acute in discrete-event project-scheduling simulations, where sequential state updates, nested loops, conditional evaluations, and object-oriented structures limit the benefits of compiled numerical and GPU-accelerated libraries. Addressing these bottlenecks typically requires iterative profiling, refactoring, testing, and validation, yet researchers may lack the time or specialized software-engineering expertise for low-level optimization. This paper presents a systematic refactoring approach using Claude agentic AI on real-world project-scheduling workloads in a high-performance computing (HPC) environment. Guided by representative benchmarks and correctness checks, the agent identifies bottlenecks, implements targeted optimizations, and evaluates their effects, while the researcher retains final control. Testing runtime reduced from 1,298 seconds to under 200 seconds without changing outputs, saving four million core-hours (NZ\$320,000) annually.

</details>


### [72] [An Agentic RAG and Evaluation Framework for Assurance Case Generation: Industrial Use Case for the EU Cyber Resilience Act Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19509)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Iker Lasa Ojanguren,Dusica Marijan,Maria I. Maslioukova,José Arias Marin,Pavlos Kosmides*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于agentic检索增强生成（RAG）的自动化框架，用于生成符合欧盟《网络弹性法案》（CRA）的保证案例（Assurance Cases, ACs），并通过形式化的Claim-Argument-Evidence逻辑进行支撑，显著降低了SME在网络安全合规评估中的资源负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟CRA合规对中小企业（SME）而言是一项资源密集型挑战，其复杂度主要来源于网络安全一致性评估的繁琐性。然而，合规评估对于证明监管合规性以及确保产品安全与弹性至关重要，因此亟需一种自动化、可扩展且可解释的解决方案来减轻SME的合规负担。

Method: 该方法构建了一个代理式检索增强生成（agentic RAG）框架，基于形式化的Claim-Argument-Evidence（主张-论证-证据）逻辑，通过系统化映射技术文档要求来生成保证案例。同时引入自然语言推理（NLI）评估器用于验证证据的grounding与可追溯性。

Result: 在Catalink的PATROLIoT野火监测系统案例研究中，该框架生成了70个保证案例，具有较高的grounding密度（每个AC约4.4个构件）；NLI评估器达到0.88的准确率，专家验证的合理性评分达3.06，表明其具备可靠的证据grounding和可解释的论证支持。

Conclusion: 该工作为自动化CRA合规评估提供了一种可扩展、可解释的实践方案，在减少人工工作量的同时保持透明的决策支持，对从业者具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Complying with the EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) is a resource-intensive challenge for SMEs due to the complexity of cybersecurity conformity assessment. Yet, it is essential for demonstrating regulatory compliance and ensuring product security and resilience. To address this, we introduce an automated framework for generating Assurance Cases (ACs) using an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation grounded in a formal Claim-Argument-Evidence logic. By systematically mapping technical documentation requirements, the framework streamlines the generation of certification evidence. We validate our approach on a case study of Catalink's PATROLIoT wildfire monitoring system, where the agentic RAG generated 70 ACs with high grounding density (~4.4 artefacts per AC). The proposed Natural Language Inference (NLI) evaluator achieves 0.88 accuracy, which provides robust evidence grounding and traceability, while expert-validated plausibility (3.06) supports interpretable justifications. For practitioners, this work provides a scalable, interpretable approach for automating mandatory CRA conformity assessments, reducing manual effort while maintaining transparent decision support.

</details>

*Tomohiro Oda,Han-Myung Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在VDM规范中记录和利用执行轨迹（包括赋值、操作调用和返回语句），将操作内部行为作为基于状态的模型持久化和分析，并在ViennaTalk中实现事件数据模型用于可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM通过数学定理证明和模拟执行进行验证，动画解释器和调试器虽能支持验证和逐步执行，但操作内部行为缺乏可持久化分析和可视化的手段。

Method: 提出记录执行轨迹（赋值、操作调用、返回语句），设计事件数据模型，并在ViennaTalk中实现，用于可视化分析。

Result: 在ViennaTalk中实现了执行轨迹的事件数据模型，并应用于可视化展示。

Conclusion: 通过记录执行轨迹并将操作内部行为建模为基于状态的模型，可增强VDM规范的验证与分析能力。

Abstract: VDM has been pursuing rigorous verification through mathematical theorem proving and software testing via simulated execution. Animation through an interpreter enables validation of the specification to ensure it meets the required functionality. Step-by-step execution in a debugger also allows the user to follow the internal behavior of operations. In this paper, we propose the recording and utilization of execution traces of assignments, operation calls, and return statements to make the internal behavior of operations persistent and analyzable as state-based models. The data model of events in execution traces, its implementation in ViennaTalk, and its application to visualization will be introduced.

</details>


### [74] [Garbage Collection and Energy Consumption in Java: A Controlled Study Across Workloads and JDKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19520)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对Java应用的三种垃圾回收器（Serial、Parallel、G1）在三个应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK与Oracle JDK）下进行了受控经验评估。结果显示Parallel平均能耗最低（839.8 J），但ANOVA分析未确立统计上可靠的回收器效应；工作负载强度才是能耗的显著驱动因素，且OpenJDK与Oracle JDK间无显著能耗差异，不支持存在普遍最优的垃圾回收器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾回收器的选择是一个低成本配置决策，能显著影响Java应用的性能与能耗，但聚合的能耗排名是否能在异构应用、工作负载强度和JDK发行版之间泛化仍不清楚。

Method: 采用受控经验评估方法，在三个Java应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK与Oracle JDK）下评估Serial、Parallel和G1三种垃圾回收器；使用EnergiBridge测量处理器封装能耗和执行时间，并推导补充的能耗-性能指标；采用RCBD（随机完全区组设计）ANOVA进行统计分析。

Result: Parallel记录最低平均能耗（839.8 J），Serial紧随其后（857.6 J），G1最高（969.0 J）；但RCBD ANOVA未确立统计上可靠的回收器效应。工作负载强度是显著的能耗驱动因素，重度负载能耗远高于轻/中度负载。能耗与执行时间呈中等正相关（r=0.33），OpenJDK与Oracle JDK间无显著能耗差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果不支持存在普遍能耗最优的垃圾回收器。工作负载强度是管理Java能耗更可靠的杠杆，垃圾回收器的选择应作为基于目标系统测量支持的应用特定调优决策，而非依赖聚合排名。

Abstract: Garbage-collector selection is a low-effort configuration decision that can influence both the performance and energy consumption of Java applications. However, it remains unclear whether aggregate energy rankings among collectors generalise across heterogeneous applications, workload intensities, and JDK distributions. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of Serial, Parallel, and G1 garbage collection across three Java applications, three workload intensities, and two JDK distributions: OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Using EnergiBridge, processor-package energy consumption and execution time were measured and complementary energy-performance metrics derived. Across all evaluated configurations, Parallel recorded the lowest mean energy consumption (839.8 J), followed closely by Serial (857.6 J) and G1 (969.0 J), but an RCBD ANOVA did not establish a statistically reliable collector effect. Workload intensity, by contrast, was a significant driver of energy consumption, with heavy workloads consuming substantially more energy than light or medium workloads regardless of collector. Energy consumption showed a moderate positive association with execution time (r = 0.33), indicating that longer-running configurations tended toward higher energy use, though the relationship was far from proportional. No statistically significant energy difference was found between OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Overall, the results do not support a universally energy-optimal garbage collector; instead, workload intensity emerges as the more reliable lever for managing Java energy consumption, and collector selection should be treated as an application-specific tuning decision supported by measurement on the target system rather than aggregate rankings alone.

</details>


### [75] [Auditing and Decomposing Feedback-Driven Evolution in LLM Test Generation under the Oracle Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19626)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了反馈驱动的LLM测试生成中"执行反馈=自验证信号"这一假设的失败模式。通过142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个保留任务的系统性审计发现：单个接受程序作为ground truth会导致27.79%-50.12%的生成输出与多实现专家面板不一致，单参考oracle使演化增益被高估9.46-14.85个百分点，且真实三轮反馈循环与安慰剂对照无显著差异。作者提出audit-and-placebo协议以分离验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反馈驱动测试生成研究普遍将执行反馈视为自验证信号，默认在单个接受程序上执行生成输入并以其输出作为ground truth。这种单参考oracle范式可能因无效或欠规范输入而产生虚假的故障检测和虚假的演化收益，但缺乏系统性审计来量化这一失败模式的严重程度。

Method: 采用"审计与安慰剂"协议：(1) 在142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个保留任务上，使用两个代码模型、三个种子和错误交叉拟合的真实提交进行大规模实验；(2) 构建三个已接受实现协商一致的专家面板作为参考；(3) 对比单参考oracle与面板oracle下的演化增益；(4) 设计密度匹配的安慰剂对照（Placebo）与真实三轮反馈循环对比；(5) 由两位软件工程博士生进行盲法语义审计。

Result: 在三个已接受实现一致的输入上，单参考生成输出与面板匹配率仅27.79%和50.12%。单参考oracle将演化增益高估9.46-14.85个百分点；审计后，等预算独立重采样比基于变异的演化好6.01-18.83点。真实与安慰剂的外部差异为+0.13和-0.50点，保留差异为+1.99和+0.28点，不构成细粒度反馈益处的稳健证据。盲法语义审计中94.41%的面板不确认为无效，3.60%为有效。

Conclusion: 单参考执行反馈作为ground truth不可靠，会产生虚假的故障检测和夸大的演化收益；反馈循环的"真实收益"在很大程度上源于额外采样而非真正的语义反馈。研究者应使用audit-and-placebo协议来区分验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈归因，避免在自我演化测试生成器的评估中得到误导性结论。

Abstract: Execution feedback is often treated as a self-verifying signal for improving LLM-generated tests. However, when generated inputs are executed on a single accepted program and its outputs are used as ground truth, invalid or underspecified inputs can create spurious fault detections and apparent evolutionary gains. We audit this failure mode in feedback-driven test generation using 142 development tasks, 114 locked external tasks, and 138 held-out tasks, with two code models, three seeds, and fault-cross-fitted real submissions. On external inputs for which three accepted implementations agree, generated outputs match the panel on only 27.79% and 50.12% of cases. A single-reference oracle inflates the measured gain from evolution by 9.46-14.85 percentage points; after auditing, equal-budget independent resampling outperforms mutation-based evolution by 6.01-18.83 points. We further compare a genuine three-round feedback loop with a density-matched placebo. External Real-Placebo differences are +0.13 and -0.50 points, while held-out differences are +1.99 and +0.28 points and do not provide robust evidence of fine-grained feedback benefit. A blinded semantic audit by two software engineering doctoral students classifies 94.41% of panel-disconfirmed inputs as invalid but 3.60% as valid, showing that panel disagreement is informative but not semantic proof. We propose an audit-and-placebo protocol that separates verifier artifacts, interaction scaffolding, and grounded feedback credit in evaluations of self-evolving test generators.

</details>


### [76] [Loreley: Repository-Scale Program Evolution with Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19703)
*Mohan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Loreley是一个将完整仓库状态保存在质量-多样性（QD）存档中、从中采样父代或提供编辑上下文顺序搜索系统。在匹配的Zstandard实验中，QD策略在48个任务时比Sequential Champion低0.135%，比Independent Root高0.320%，两种对比均未确立QD优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统顺序智能体搜索会丢弃备选分支，独立提案虽保持广度但需从根重启。研究者希望探索能否通过保留完整仓库状态的质量-多样性存档来兼顾探索广度与路径连续性，从而获得更好的搜索终点性能。

Method: Loreley将Git提交作为候选解，在隔离worktree中产生，由项目提供的评估器评判；采用QD存档保留完整仓库状态，从中采样父代或作为编辑上下文。实验采用七组配对块、每组48个物理候选任务（共1,008个），以根初始化、各策略原生并发度运行，在预算检查点选择胜者，并用隐藏的独立评审集（holdout）评估固定候选。同时进行回顾性单持有者规则分析QD流祖先路径。

Result: 在48个任务时，QD比Sequential Champion低0.135%（95% BCa区间：-0.556%至+0.161%），比Independent Root高0.320%（区间：-0.082%至+0.686%），均无显著优势。Sequential拥有最高的48任务平均和中位数。QD存档保留和后续采样确实发生；七个最终QD胜者中四个具有非现任状态的父系祖先，计入inspiration边后升至六个，但未能证明提供上下文直接导致编辑。更早的三次能力战役在Python库和Zstandard修订中产生了第四代多文件改进。

Conclusion: Loreley确实触发了预期的垫脚石（stepping-stone）机制，存档中的非现任状态被采样并参与祖先形成，但在受控实验中，48个工作预算下QD并未展现终点优势，三种策略的差异在统计上不可区分或略偏于Sequential Champion。

Abstract: Sequential agent search accumulates changes from its current champion but discards alternative branches; independent proposals preserve breadth but restart from the root. Loreley instead retains complete repository states in a Quality-Diversity (QD) archive and samples them as parents or supplies them as context for later edits. Candidates are Git commits produced in isolated worktrees and judged by a project-supplied evaluator. We compare configured Loreley QD, sequential champion editing, and independent root proposals in a matched Zstandard experiment: seven paired blocks and 48 physical candidate jobs per policy and block (1,008 total), with root-only initialization and each policy's native concurrency. Validation selected a winner at each budget checkpoint; an agent-hidden holdout measured the fixed candidate. At 48 jobs, QD was 0.135% below Sequential Champion (95% BCa interval for the paired effect: -0.556% to +0.161%) and 0.320% above Independent Root (-0.082% to +0.686%). Neither contrast established a QD advantage; Sequential had the highest observed 48-job mean and median. Archive retention and later sampling did occur. Four of seven final QD winners had a non-incumbent state in their primary-parent ancestry under a retrospective one-incumbent rule applied only to the observed QD stream. Including inspiration edges raised the count to six, without showing that supplied context caused an edit. Three earlier capability campaigns produced generation-4, multi-file improvements in two Python libraries and a separate Zstandard revision. Loreley engaged the intended stepping-stone mechanism, but the controlled experiment did not show an endpoint benefit at 48 jobs.

</details>


### [77] [A Fully Automated, Deployment-Aware Testing Pipeline for IoT-Based Automotive Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19752)
*Denesa Zyberaj,Roman Vintonyak,Pascal Hirmer,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端、支持部署感知的汽车嵌入式软件测试流水线，结合LLM/VLM辅助的测试与代码生成、人在环审核，以及基于Eclipse openDuT的分布式部署能力，在儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）案例上实现了100%的功能需求覆盖率和Gherkin生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆中的嵌入式软件测试面临系统复杂度高、架构分散以及严格安全性和性能约束等挑战，传统测试方法难以应对OEM与供应商之间的跨组织、跨地域协同测试需求。

Method: 提出一个端到端、部署感知的测试流水线，由三部分组成：(1)需求驱动的测试与代码生成，借助大语言模型（LLM）和视觉语言模型（VLM）辅助；(2)人在环审核机制，减少人工负担并提高一致性；(3)基于Eclipse openDuT支持在地理分散的网络物理系统和IoT基础设施上进行灵活分布式部署，优化节点可用性和跨组织协调。

Result: 在儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）案例研究中，该流水线实现了全部9条需求的功能覆盖，受控需求集上的Gherkin生成准确率达100%；通过Eclipse openDuT在跨地域的ECU上进行的分布式测试执行验证了其在OEM-供应商测试工作流中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该端到端测试流水线有效结合了生成式AI能力与人在环审核及分布式测试基础设施，为现代汽车嵌入式软件的复杂测试需求提供了可行方案，尤其在OEM与供应商的跨组织协同测试场景中表现出良好的适用性。

Abstract: Testing embedded software in modern vehicles is challenging due to system complexity, decentralized architectures, and strict safety and performance constraints. In this work, we present an end-to-end, deployment-aware testing pipeline for IoT-based automotive applications. The pipeline combines requirement-driven test and code generation with large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) assistance, and human-in-the-loop curation to reduce manual effort and improve consistency. Using Eclipse openDuT, it supports flexible, distributed deployment across geographically separated cyber-physical and IoT infrastructures, optimizing for node availability and cross-organizational coordination. For validation, we conduct a case study using a Child Presence Detection System (CPDS), achieving full functional requirement coverage across all 9 requirements and 100% Gherkin generation accuracy on the controlled requirement set. Distributed test execution across geographically separated ECUs via Eclipse openDuT confirms the pipeline's applicability to OEM--supplier testing workflows.

</details>


### [78] [PRAXIS: Graph-Grounded Tacit Knowledge for Domain Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19784)
*Xue Jiang,Tianyu Zhang,Lingwei Wu,Ziyu Wang,Ge Li,Yuan Sui,Hao Zhu,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAXIS框架，通过模拟人类开发流程来系统性地提取、表示和复用领域代码中的隐性知识，从而提升LLM代理在领域特定代码生成任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在通用软件工程任务上表现优异，但在领域特定代码生成上表现不佳。作者识别出根本原因在于代理缺乏隐性知识——包括领域业务规则、接口契约和操作惯例——这些知识开发者通过实践内化但从未文档化，且深埋在领域代码中，分散于代码实体及其依赖关系之间，难以检索或学习。

Method: PRAXIS框架通过三个步骤工作：(1) 在目标代码库中模拟人类开发工作流程来获取隐性知识；(2) 将获取的知识蒸馏为结构化单元，并组织在代码依赖图上；(3) 在代码交互点主动向代理呈现这些知识。

Result: 大量实验表明，PRAXIS优于配备强大代理搜索能力的SOTA代理，以及基于经验和基于技能的方法。该框架能无缝集成到各种代理框架和LLM中并带来一致的性能提升，且支持持续演化，性能随着实践积累稳定扩展。

Conclusion: PRAXIS通过解决LLM代理在领域代码生成中缺乏隐性知识的问题，提供了一种系统化的知识提取与复用框架，具有广泛的框架兼容性、一致的性能提升和持续的演化能力。

Abstract: LLM agents have achieved strong performance on general software engineering tasks, yet struggle with domain-specific code generation. We identify the root cause as the agent's lack of tacit knowledge, including domain-specific business rules, interface contracts, and operational conventions that developers internalize through practice but never document. This knowledge is deeply buried beneath the domain code, dispersed across code entities and their dependency relations, and invisible to the agent that lacks it. These properties make tacit knowledge inherently difficult to retrieve or learn. In this work, we propose PRAXIS, a framework that enables agents to systematically extract, represent, and reuse tacit knowledge for domain code generation. PRAXIS acquires tacit knowledge by simulating human development workflows within the target codebase, distills it into structured units organized on the code dependency graph, and proactively surfaces it to the agent at the point of code interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PRAXIS outperforms state-of-the-art agents equipped with powerful agentic search capabilities, as well as experience-based and skill-based methods. The approach integrates seamlessly into various agent frameworks and LLMs with consistent performance improvements, and supports continual evolution with performance steadily scaling as practice accumulates.

</details>


### [79] [Further Progress Towards Operation Proof Obligation Generation for VDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19848)
*Nick Battle,Carlo Rende,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是VDM形式化方法中证明义务生成工作的更新版本，改进了循环不变量、新增循环变体、改进了操作调用的证明义务（包括隐式声明操作和规范语句），并为操作添加了递归度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM形式化方法通过证明义务来确保模型内部一致性，但原有的证明义务生成方法在循环不变量、操作调用和递归操作等方面存在不足，需要改进以提高形式化验证的准确性和完整性。

Method: 本文改进了循环不变量的处理方式，新增了循环变体（variant）以证明循环终止性；改进了操作调用的证明义务生成，涵盖隐式声明操作和规范语句；并为递归操作添加了递归度量（recursive measures）来支持终止性证明。

Result: 通过具体示例展示了上述新特性（循环不变量改进、循环变体、改进的操作调用证明义务、递归度量）在实际应用中的效果。

Conclusion: 本文是对先前工作的更新，提出的改进使得VDM证明义务生成更加完善，能够更好地支持循环终止性证明和递归操作的验证。

Abstract: The VDM formalism can ensure that models are internally consistent. Potential inconsistencies are highlighted by assertions called proof obligations. This paper is an update to the work described in [1], regarding proof obligation generation for VDM operations. We present the latest work which improves the approach to loop invariants, adds loop variants, improves proof obligations for operation calls, including implicitly declared operations and specification statements, and adds recursive measures for operations. The new features are illustrated with examples.

</details>


### [80] [Repo0: Design-Driven Zero-to-All Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19854)
*Silin Chen,Haoyi Teng,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Jiale Huang,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Repo0是一个面向零到全部代码生成任务的连续结构演化框架，通过维护双有向无环图（Dual-DAG）架构状态，在生成代码前持续演化组件边界直至结构收敛，再由收敛后的架构指导测试驱动的代码生成。在六个真实仓库上分别比最强的RPG基线提升最多20.08个百分点的功能覆盖率和29.74个百分点的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在代码生成时大多假设预定义的仓库架构，但在"零到全部"代码生成场景中，智能体需要直接从未经结构化的自然语言需求出发构建完整软件项目，同时在整个开发过程中保持模块化的仓库架构。这一假设的不成立使得现有系统在该场景下表现受限，亟需一种能够动态演化并维持架构结构的框架。

Method: Repo0提出连续结构演化框架，核心是维护显式的架构状态，即双有向无环图（Dual-DAG），包含需求级DAG、组件级DAG及二者之间的对齐关系。框架从自然语言需求出发，通过模块化度量引导的结构化操作迭代演化组件边界，直到结构收敛后，再由收敛架构指导测试驱动的代码生成。

Result: 在RepoCraft的六个真实仓库上，使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek V3.2进行评估。Repo0在所有设置下均取得最高的功能覆盖率和通过率。与最强的仓库规划基线RPG相比，功能覆盖率最多提升20.08个百分点，通过率最多提升29.74个百分点。消融实验和结构演化分析验证了Dual-DAG架构状态、模块化引导的结构演化以及显式结构收敛的重要性。

Conclusion: Repo0通过显式维护和演化架构状态，有效地解决了零到全部代码生成中仓库架构动态构建的难题。实验证明Dual-DAG架构状态、模块化引导的结构演化和显式结构收敛三者缺一不可，为大规模软件项目自动化生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents have made substantial progress in code generation, yet most existing systems assume a predefined repository architecture. This assumption does not hold in zero-to-all code generation, where an agent must construct an entire software project directly from natural-language requirements while maintaining a modular repository architecture throughout development. We present Repo0, a continuous structural evolution framework for zero-to-all code generation. Repo0 maintains an explicit architectural state instantiated as a Dual-Directed-Acyclic-Graph (Dual-DAG), consisting of a requirement-level DAG, a component-level DAG, and their alignment relation. Starting from natural-language requirements, it iteratively evolves component boundaries through structural actions guided by modularity metrics until structural convergence, after which the converged architecture guides test-driven development code generation. We evaluate Repo0 on six real-world repositories from RepoCraft using GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek V3.2. Repo0 achieves the highest Functionality Coverage and Pass Rate across all settings. Compared with RPG, the strongest repository-planning baseline, Repo0 improves Functionality Coverage by up to 20.08 percentage points and Pass Rate by up to 29.74 percentage points. Ablation and structural-evolution analyses further demonstrate the importance of the Dual-DAG architectural state, modularity-guided structural evolution, and explicit structural convergence.

</details>


### [81] [Evaluating Automated Testing on an Open-Source Web Application Using Cypress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19960)
*Quoc-Binh Nguyen,Truc-Ly Phan Nguyen,Ngoc Hong Tran,Dung Hai Dinh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了使用Cypress框架进行端到端自动化测试的有效性，通过对开源Web应用部署27个测试用例，从执行速度、可靠性和可维护性三个维度进行验证。结果表明Cypress测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合开发过程中的高频运行；大多数测试用例跨多次运行结果一致，仅少数涉及复杂交互功能的测试出现不稳定；同时数据驱动属性（data-cy）和页面对象模型（POM）架构能显著降低UI变更时的维护开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动化测试在现代Web开发中被广泛采用，但其实际效果受到执行时间、结果稳定性和测试套件可维护性等多方面因素的影响。作者希望通过实证研究系统评估Cypress框架在真实Web应用中的测试有效性，为开发团队选择合适的测试框架和测试策略提供依据。

Method: 选取一个开源Web应用作为实验对象，使用Cypress框架编写并部署27个端到端测试用例。从三个维度衡量测试有效性：执行速度（运行耗时）、可靠性（多次运行的稳定性/flakiness情况）以及可维护性（分析元素定位策略和POM架构对维护成本的影响，特别是data-cy属性在UI变更时的韧性表现）。

Result: Cypress端到端测试套件表现出执行时间短且稳定的特点，适合软件开发过程中的频繁运行。大部分测试用例在多次运行中结果一致，仅涉及复杂交互功能的少数测试出现不稳定现象。此外，采用具有韧性的data-cy元素定位属性显著减少了UI变更时的维护工作量，POM架构有助于提升测试套件的可维护性。

Conclusion: Cypress框架适用于端到端自动化测试，尤其在需要高频运行的开发场景中表现良好。但复杂交互功能的测试稳定性仍需关注；在测试设计层面，合理的元素定位策略（如data-cy）与架构设计（如POM）是降低维护成本、保障测试套件长期有效性的关键因素。

Abstract: End-to-end automated testing is increasingly used in web software development to ensure system quality and shorten response times during development. However, the true effectiveness of automated testing depends on many factors including execution time, stability of test results, and maintainability of the test suite as the application continues to evolve. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of end-to-end automated testing using the Cypress framework for an open-source web application. We deployed the experiment with 27 test cases. The test's effectiveness is measured by execution speed, reliability, and maintainability. The experimental results show that the Cypress-based end-to-end test suite has short and stable execution times. It suits frequent runs during software development. The majority of test cases achieved consistent results across multiple runs, while flakiness only occurred in a few tests which involve complex interactive functions. Furthermore, the study highlights the impact of element locator strategies and Page Object Model (POM) architecture on test suite maintainability, demonstrating that resilient data-cy attributes significantly reduce maintenance overhead when UI changes occur.

</details>


### [82] [BreakGuard: Towards Detecting Dependency Breaking Changes with LLM-Generated Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20167)
*Rachna Raj,Benoit Baudry,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BreakGuard是一种利用大型语言模型（LLMs）自动生成测试套件来检测客户端代码中库断裂性变更（BCs）的方法。通过静态提取调用目标库方法的客户端焦点方法，为每个焦点方法生成测试，并在89个真实BCs上评估，使用最优配置检测到30.3%的断裂性变更，平均每个检测到的BC成本约0.90美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在演进过程中会引入断裂性变更（BCs），破坏与客户端建立的服务契约，导致客户端应用运行时行为改变甚至崩溃。然而，客户端现有的测试套件由于库覆盖率有限，往往无法检测到这些变更。因此需要一种自动化的方法来帮助客户端检测库升级带来的断裂性变更。

Method: BreakGuard首先静态提取每个调用目标库方法（调用点）的客户端方法（焦点方法），然后为每个焦点方法生成测试。一个测试被认定为能检测到BC，需满足在断裂前版本上测试通过、在断裂版本上测试失败。实验使用3个LLM（GPT4o、Qwen3-coder-480B、GPT-OSS-120B）和三种上下文级别（最小级、方法级、类级）进行配置评估。

Result: 在BUMP数据集的89个真实断裂性变更上评估，最优配置下BreakGuard检测到30.3%的断裂性变更（27/89），平均每个检测到的BC成本约0.90美元。成功检测了不同类别的库（如JSON库、日志、解析）中的BCs，但发现LLM生成的测试对崩溃型断裂性变更的检测更为可靠，而在行为型BCs上表现相对较弱。

Conclusion: BreakGuard证明了LLM驱动的测试生成可以有效地辅助客户端检测库升级中的断裂性变更，尤其在崩溃型变更上表现出色的成本效益比。但行为型BCs的检测仍具挑战性，提示未来需要在测试生成策略上针对行为型变更做进一步优化。

Abstract: Open-source libraries play an important role in software development by providing reusable features that expedite the development process. As libraries evolve, they release new versions that add features, fix bugs, or apply security patches. In this process, they may break the contract established with their clients by introducing breaking changes (BCs) that alter the runtime behavior and break client applications. Client-side test suites often fail to detect these BCs because of limited library coverage that does not exercise all library methods used in the client's codebase.
  We propose BreakGuard, an approach that generates a test suite to detect breaking changes in clients. BreakGuard statically extracts every client method (focal method) that invokes the target library method (call site), then generates tests per focal method. A test detects a BC if it passes on the pre-breaking version and fails on the breaking version. We evaluate our approach on 89 real-world breaking changes from the BUMP dataset, using 3 LLMs (GPT4o, Qwen3-coder-480B, GPT-OSS-120B) and three context levels: minimal, method, and class. Using the best-performing configuration, BreakGuard detects 30.3% of breaking changes (27 of 89) at a mean cost of roughly $0.90 USD per detected breaking change. We successfully detected BCs from different library categories (e.g., JSON libraries, logging, parsing), but we find LLM-generated tests to be more reliable for detecting crash-type breaking changes as opposed to behavioural BCs.

</details>


### [83] [From Agent Behaviour to Agent-Friendly Documentation: An Empirical Study of How Coding Agents Discover, Read, and Write Technical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20195)
*Zhijun Gao,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过行为扎根研究，分析了两个公开数据集（SWE-chat 557个代理编码会话和AIDev 33,097个代理pull request），揭示了自主编码代理与文档交互的四个关键发现：代理主要使用面向代理的工件（指令文件和工作笔记占60.5%），文档咨询与代码编辑的关系无法确定，缺乏基于文档的验证序列且咨询与即时测试负相关，以及咨询多为自主发起而非失败驱动。研究提出代理-文档交互是"双叶循环"而非线性过程，并发现"可操作性"和"可验证性"这两个"代理友好"文档的假设属性缺乏一致的行为支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发的文档是为人编写的，但越来越多的代码变更由自主编码代理完成。代理查阅哪些文档、何时查阅、以及查阅后产生什么后果完全是未知的。理解代理-文档交互行为对于改进文档设计、提升代理软件开发的效率和质量至关重要。

Method: 采用行为扎根研究方法，分析两个公开数据集：1) SWE-chat中的557个代理编码会话（94,813个开发事件，含3,033个文档交互）；2) AIDev中的33,097个代理pull request（690,260条文件级变更记录）。使用相邻转移概率、提升度（lift）、阶段调整模型（OR值）和聚类置信区间等统计方法，并开发了描述代理-文档交互的模型。

Result: 四项主要发现：(1) 代理的文档工作以面向代理的工件为主，指令文件和工作笔记占60.5%，传统技术文档仅占10.6%，API参考仅占1.3%；(2) 文档咨询与代码编辑间的因果关系不明确，相邻转移概率仅0.002，未调整邻近度1.05但阶段调整后OR为1.33；(3) 未观察到显式的基于文档的验证序列，咨询与即时测试呈负相关（lift 0.23，调整后OR 0.39）；(4) 咨询多为自主发起（70.2%）而非失败驱动（7.5%），且文档变更紧随代码变更（代码先被触及的频率是文档的4.7倍）。

Conclusion: 代理-文档交互呈现"双叶循环"模式而非线性流程。传统文档实践的两个核心假设——"可操作性"和"可验证性"——缺乏一致的行为证据支持。研究公开了完整的流水线、编码方案和事件级数据，为后续研究"代理友好"文档设计提供了数据基础。

Abstract: Technical documentation is written for human developers, but an increasing share of software changes is now authored by autonomous coding agents. Which documents they consult, when, and what follows remain unknown. We conduct a behaviour-grounded study of agent-documentation interaction across two public datasets: 557 agentic coding sessions from SWE-chat, yielding 94,813 development events including 3,033 documentation interactions; and 33,097 agentic pull requests from AIDev, with 690,260 classified file-level change records. Four findings challenge current documentation practice. First, agents' documentation work is dominated by agent-facing artefacts: instruction files and working notes account for 60.5% of all documentation interactions, versus 10.6% for classical technical documentation and 1.3% for API references. Second, the link between consultation and code editing is unresolved: the adjacent transition probability is 0.002 and the unadjusted three-event lift 1.05, whereas a stage-adjusted model places it above unity (OR 1.33 [1.09, 1.62]); documentation creation is elevated unadjusted (lift 1.67) but its adjusted interval includes unity. Third, no explicit documentation-based validation sequence was observed, and consultation is associated with less immediate testing (lift 0.23, cluster CI 0.08-0.45; adjusted OR 0.39 [0.25, 0.60]). Fourth, consultation is self-initiated (70.2%) far more often than failure-driven (7.5%), and documentation trails code: among multi-commit pull requests changing both, code is touched first 4.7x more often. From these traces we derive a descriptive model of agent-documentation interaction as a two-lobed cycle rather than a linear journey, and show that two widely assumed properties of "agent-friendly" documentation - actionability and verifiability - lack consistent behavioural support. We release our pipeline, coding scheme, and event-level data.

</details>

### [84] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI代理的上下文获取过程形式化为主动推理（active inference）问题：代理需要在默认假设与花费token的澄清/检索/工具调用之间做权衡，通过内层推理更新潜在任务状态的信念，外层决策选择上下文动作以最小化期望自由能（含代价）。在确定性场景下认知项退化为期望信息增益（可归一化token成本），并实例化为最优提问框架（OQA），在25-300个候选的二元/多元分类任务上基准测试前沿语言模型，同时研究了生成前澄清和token预算下的自动prompt优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI代理需要在获取上下文（如约束、偏好、文件、任务变量）时尽可能高效。当用户省略信息时，代理要么采用默认假设，要么花token去澄清/检索/调用工具/尝试prompt。现有方法缺少对这一权衡的统一形式化框架，本文旨在提供一个模型无关的设计原则来指导代理的上下文获取层。

Method: 将上下文获取建模为主动推理：内层推理步骤更新关于隐藏任务状态的信念（后验），外层决策在内层推理基础上选择下一步动作（上下文动作/任务动作/停止），以最小化期望自由能（含成本项）。确定性场景下认知项退化为依据token成本归一化的期望信息增益。实例化为最优提问（OQA）框架，采用精确后验和动态规划作为oracle，并在25到300个候选类别的二元及多类别分类任务中基准测试前沿语言模型。

Result: 在二元和多路分类任务（25-300候选）上，用OQA作为最优oracle对前沿语言模型进行基准测试；同时探索了生成前澄清以及token预算约束下的自动化prompt优化。结果表明该框架能够有效建模并指导上下文获取中的信息-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 该形式化是模型无关的，将主动推理视为AI代理上下文获取层的设计原则，为理解代理在信息获取与资源消耗间的权衡提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [85] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个聚焦于不确定环境下基于群体的鲁棒元启发式算法用于泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的系统性综述，从机制导向角度组织现有方法，并提出基准测试套件与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP是港口码头运营中的代表性调度问题，在到达偏差、装卸时间波动和资源中断等不确定性下，基于名义假设优化的调度方案在执行中可能变得脆弱。尽管群体元启发式算法已被广泛用于BACAP，但现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实证评估协议方面较为分散，缺乏系统性综述。

Method: 本文采用机制导向的组织方式：(1)总结BACAP中的不确定性来源与信息表示；(2)从解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动力学、可行性保持与恢复四个维度组织现有方法；(3)构建不确定BACAP基准测试套件，并报告代表性元启发式算法与不同鲁棒性策略组合的基线结果。

Result: 提供了一个首个聚焦不确定BACAP下鲁棒群体元启发式算法的综述框架，建立了基准测试套件并给出插画性基线实验结果，系统梳理了机制层面的方法分类。

Conclusion: 识别出四项开放挑战：基准扩展、鲁棒性感知的搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性、以及非平稳不确定性，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [86] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出从"AI是否有意识"这一不可解的问题转向"AI是否具有效价状态"这一可解问题，以此为基础为可能具有知觉的AI建立负责任的开发路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关于人工意识存在深层不确定性的情况下，对待可能具有知觉的AI存在两难困境：假设无意识可能伤害值得道德考量的实体，假设有意识则可能浪费资源于无意识的机器。AI意识问题的不可解性使这一困境难以摆脱。

Method: 提出一种思维转换：放弃不可解的AI意识问题，转而关注可解的AI效价（valence）问题。具体而言，评估AI是否具有那些如果它是有意识的就会构成效价体验的状态。作者论证这种效价评估足以奠定对有潜在意识的AI进行负责任的开发的基础。

Result: 论文展示了将问题从AI意识重新框架为AI效价，能够为负责任地开发可能具有意识的AI提供充分的基础，从而绕开意识问题的不可解性困境。

Conclusion: 通过将关注的焦点从不可解的AI意识问题转向可解的AI效价评估问题，可以为潜在地具有知觉的AI建立负责任的开发原则，从而在不确定条件下做出更合理的伦理决策。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [87] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"（bounded sovereignty）概念，指在API/托管端点部署前沿模型时，部署者仅拥有跨数据、模型、基础设施与交互层部分的技术与契约访问权限。论文构建四层访问类型学、按层协议需求矩阵，并提出"主权折扣成本"概念，通过135万次合成案例的消融实验和匿名国家支付基础设施场景阐明各层访问条件如何决定控制协议的可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究假设部署者可全面干预模型及其周边管线，但受监管机构通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型时，部署者往往无法控制模型权重、服务基础设施、内部追踪、更新流程或完整交互日志。这一访问权限缺失导致许多控制协议在实践中无法执行，因此需要系统性地理解部分访问条件对控制协议可行性的影响。

Method: 论文方法包括：(1) 构建跨数据、模型、基础设施、交互层的四层访问类型学；(2) 提出协议-按层需求矩阵，映射各控制协议对各层访问条件的依赖；(3) 提出"主权折扣成本"概念以量化因访问缺失而替代投入的成本；(4) 执行135万条合成案例的访问消融实验；(5) 通过匿名的国家支付基础设施场景进行解释性案例分析。

Result: 实验（作为构念效度练习而非真实支付系统证据）显示：完整日志可改善诊断准确性；预执行网关（pre-execution gateway）可支持干预；追踪访问与模型版本控制可强化事后解释能力；范围限制（scope restriction）可在提升安全性的同时降低有用性。

Conclusion: 控制协议若被提出为通用安全方案，应明确说明其访问假设。不同访问条件组合决定了协议的可执行性，受监管组织采用AI安全方案时必须结合自身实际拥有的各层访问权限进行可行性评估。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [88] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于敏感度的社交网络框架，利用姿态计算机视觉管道从商业奶牛场挤奶前区域7小时39分钟的视频中提取奶牛个体间互动，将其分类为亲和与敌对事件，构建群体层面的网络表示。经质检保留1,183个互动（36头奶牛、177对），审计样本中人工与自动标注一致率为82.8%，宏观F1为0.872。聚合网络连通，且按预测效价分层后产生明显不同的亲和/敌对网络层。作者指出合并计数会掩盖网络的行为构成，敏感度感知分析为测试竞争、亲和与福利相关变化的假设提供框架，但需纵向验证方可作为福利或健康指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化牲畜监测将行为视为孤立事件，忽略了社会关系在资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性中的核心作用。作者旨在构建一个能区分亲和与敌对互动的网络分析框架，以更全面地捕捉群体层面的社会动态。

Method: 采用姿态计算机视觉管道分析奶牛场挤奶前区域的连续视频（7小时39分钟），提取候选互动；经质量控制保留1,183个互动（36头奶牛、177个二元对）。对自动检测的互动按预测效价（affiliative/agonistic）分类，构建加权网络；通过网络分析（密度、传递性、平均路径长度、社区检测、中心性指标）描述群体结构，并将亲和与敌对网络层进行对比。

Result: 审计样本（198个片段）中自动与人工标注一致率为82.8%，未加权审计样本宏观F1为0.872。聚合网络连通（密度0.281；传递性0.513；平均路径长度1.88），预测亲和事件形成5个算法社区（模块度Q=0.429）。观察区内预测敌对互动占保留事件72.4%、互动时长76.0%。拥有最多互动伙伴的奶牛并非中介中心性最高者。按预测效价分层后，亲和与敌对网络层在边集合、社区划分和个体位置上均呈现明显差异。

Conclusion: 合并互动计数会掩盖网络的行为构成；敏感度感知分析能区分亲和与敌对网络层，为测试关于竞争、亲和和福利相关变化的假设提供新框架。但该框架需经过纵向验证，方可作为福利或健康指标使用。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [89] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估了大语言模型（LLM）能否生成操作上真实的空中交通管制（ATC）通话。以旧金山"Bay Tour"航线的一次通用航空飞行为基准（P0），通过飞行员在环过程设计了五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），并嵌入有状态的多轮对话管道中。在九个开源和闭源LLM上，系统性地变化提示、上下文示例以及历史条件来源（自身回复或注入的真实历史）。结果表明：提供工作示例能提升相似度，但收紧提示反而有害——最宽松的提示表现最好，而脚本化最强的提示因其自身错误在多轮对话中累积而崩溃，注入正确历史可修复该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中交通管制（ATC）通信是安全关键的对话，即使空中交通管理的其他部分已实现半自动化，该对话仍主要依赖人类完成。作者希望探索LLM能否生成操作上真实的ATC通话，为未来LLM辅助的ATC系统铺路。

Method: 使用一次实验性通用航空飞行（旧金山Bay Tour航线）的手工转录文本作为基准（P0）。通过飞行员在环过程设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态的多轮管道中，模型扮演ATC角色对固定飞行员转录进行回应，并以累积对话历史为条件。在九个开源和闭源LLM上，变化提示结构、是否提供另一实验飞行的转录作为上下文示例，以及模型是否条件于自身先前的回复或注入的真实历史。使用词法、结构、语义相似度指标以及经人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）对回合进行评分。

Result: 提供工作示例能一致地提升相似度得分，但收紧提示并未提升性能——最宽松的提示表现最佳，而最重度脚本化的提示因其自身错误在多轮对话中累积而崩溃。向模型中注入正确的历史可以修复这种崩溃。

Conclusion: 这些结果勾勒出通往LLM辅助ATC的一条具体路径，同时也指出了目前的局限性。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [90] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors（结果监视器），用于检测工具调用结果中违反"结果契约"（outcome contracts）的情况。这些契约从任务无关的轨迹中挖掘或从公共模式派生。当检测到违规时，监视器保留结果并发出非绑定收据，指明违规属性及公开恢复工具。实验表明该机制在ToolMaze任务中将完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%（四个模型、两个提供商家族），在tau-bench零售任务中两个层级分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点。控制实验证明恢复工具列表是效果的关键来源，而检测诊断细节和时序无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工具调用超时是显式失败，agent可感知并绕行；但缓存错误页面或负价格等结果以预期格式返回且被当作事实消费，造成静默失败。这类"格式正确但语义错误"的工具结果是现有agent系统难以检测和防御的盲区。

Method: 1) 从任务无关轨迹（task-disjoint traces）中挖掘结果契约，或从公共模式（public schemas）派生契约；2) 构建Outcome Monitors检测结果违反契约；3) 违规时保留原始结果（preserves the result），同时发出非绑定收据（nonbinding receipt），收据包含违规属性名称和公开恢复工具列表；4) 在冻结的预设评估中进行注入故障实验验证。

Result: ToolMaze完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%（四个模型、两个provider家族），并在第三家复制；tau-bench零售两个层级分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点。控制实验显示：移除恢复工具列表消除增益，恢复列表则恢复效果；诊断细节与时序无显著差异。增益集中在故障阻碍完成的场景。在从发布的事件分类法转录的扩展套件上，契约词汇表之外的检测率降至46%，但交付继续且完成率不变。

Conclusion: Outcome Monitors能有效提升agent在静默工具失败场景下的任务完成率，且恢复工具列表（收据中的主动内容）是效果的主要贡献者。然而，检测能力目前局限于已挖掘/派生的契约词汇表，扩展到词汇表之外（开放集检测）仍是未来方向。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出R2-OPD框架，通过在策略蒸馏中构建两组轨迹内推理片段排名（教师奖励排名与独立估计的进步奖励排名），当两者不一致时抑制蒸馏奖励，以解决教师奖励与真实推理进展冲突的问题，从而提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略蒸馏（OPD）中，教师奖励被隐式假设为推理进展的合适代理，所有教师反馈被同等对待。然而实际中教师奖励常与真实推理进展冲突——推理步虽有明显进展，但因偏离教师输出而获得较低奖励。这种不匹配会误导策略优化。

Method: R2-OPD方法构建两类轨迹内推理片段排名：(1) 基于教师奖励的排名；(2) 基于独立估计的进步奖励的排名。当两组排名不一致时，选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，从而减少与推理进展冲突的监督信号，同时保留有效的教师引导。

Result: 与标准OPD相比，R2-OPD在推理性能上表现出一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过奖励过滤机制缓解教师奖励与推理进展之间的偏差，可以有效提升策略蒸馏的推理能力，验证了选择性利用教师反馈而非全部采纳的价值。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [92] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个正式框架和实现，用于记录小型科学研究社区部署的AI代理操作，提供长期不可篡改的代理驱动研究活动历史，保存可审计的分析、假设、数据和科学论述轨迹，使代理能基于先前工作构建并支持目的相关的信任评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究社区在快速演进的环境中会使用多样化的AI系统，需要一种机制来记录和保留AI代理驱动的研究活动历史，以便代理能在先前工作的基础上继续构建，同时保留研究者和代理进行信任评估所需的证据。

Method: Symposium通过将科学社区的持久历史与操作该历史的代理及其他系统分离开来，作为一个独立框架运行。它提供长期不可篡改的代理驱动研究活动记录，捕获科学论证的结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设以及关于哪些材料可被用作证据的明确声明，并提供发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档的可用实现。

Result: 论文提供了一个可工作的Symposium实现，包括发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档，使用户能够快速搭建和运行自己的Symposium社区。

Conclusion: Symposium区别于AI共同科学家代理或集成AI研究环境，它作为一个独立框架，将社区的持久历史与运行于其上的AI系统分离，以适应快速演化的AI技术生态，同时确保研究活动的可审计性和可追溯性。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [93] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遥测的自适应上下文压缩策略，用于边缘RAG系统。通过测量NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上的压缩权衡，发现生成阶段占据RAG预算的90%左右，中间压缩率可在几乎不损失质量的情况下将GPU能耗降低53.2%、SoC能耗降低48.2%，并主张运行时策略根据工作负载特征和边缘设备实时状态动态管理压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能提升语言模型响应质量，但检索到的上下文会加长提示词，增加prefill计算量、KV缓存占用、内存流量、延迟和能耗。虽然上下文压缩是自然解决方案，但现有方法通常采用固定压缩预算或离线选定的压缩率，忽视了工作负载变化和边缘设备的实时状态。在边缘SoC上，压缩本身也消耗延迟和能耗，可能抵消生成阶段的节省，因此需要自适应压缩策略。

Method: 作者在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor平台上，使用Llama和Qwen生成器、Natural Questions和HotpotQA数据集以及LLMLingua-2压缩方法，系统测量了不同压缩率下的延迟和能耗权衡。通过探索压缩率对GPU能耗、SoC能耗和推理质量的影响，识别出自适应操作区域，并据此提出由工作负载特征和边缘遥测驱动的运行时动态压缩管理策略。

Result: 实验表明：对于7B-8B规模的生成模型，生成阶段占据每查询延迟的约90%和GPU能耗的91%。压缩率的影响呈现出自适应操作区域：温和压缩可能错失能耗优化机会，过度压缩则会损害推理质量。中间压缩率可降低GPU能耗高达53.2%，降低SoC能耗高达48.2%，且质量损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 作者主张放弃静态固定的压缩预算，转而采用由工作负载特征和边缘设备遥测信息（实时状态）共同指导的运行时自适应压缩策略，以在边缘RAG系统中实现延迟与能耗的最优权衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [94] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman动力系统的黑盒LLM安全分类方法，通过将提示词和响应的嵌入投影到高维空间，分别拟合安全与不安全两种状态下的Koopman预测模型，并利用差分残差分数进行分类。实验表明，引入提示词嵌入能提升交互依赖类违规的检测效果，尤其是配合Llama-3等因果解码器时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中部署日益广泛，但其生成有毒、有害或违反政策内容的风险显著。在黑盒场景下高效检测这些不安全输出仍是开放挑战。现有安全检测方法多依赖分类器或规则，缺乏对提示词与响应之间动态交互关系的建模能力。

Method: 将先前用于幻觉检测的动力学系统框架扩展到LLM安全分类。具体步骤：(1) 将提示词和响应分别投影到高维嵌入空间；(2) 针对安全与不安全两种状态分别拟合Koopman算子作为预测模型；(3) 提出差分残差分数（differential residual score），通过比较两种状态下的预测误差来判断输出是否安全；(4) 关键创新在于同时纳入提示词和响应的嵌入动力学，以捕捉两者的交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准和三个嵌入模型上评估，结果显示：引入提示词嵌入带来一致的性能提升，特别是对于依赖交互的违规检测，在配合Llama-3等因果解码器时效果最佳；而仅依赖响应的违规类型则更受益于稠密语义嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了动力学系统方法可有效用于分析AI系统安全，而非传统上仅用AI建模动力学系统。这开启了以动力学视角进行AI安全分析的崭新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [95] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出科学数据技能（SciDSK），一种面向AI智能体的数据集表示方法，将数据集特定知识和操作指南封装为可复用的智能体技能，并通过科学数据技能库平台发布，实验表明其能提升智能体驱动的数据集发现与解读能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究数据越来越多地被AI智能体使用，但现有数据集表示方式难以支持自主发现、解读和调用。这一局限源于科学数据分散在异构存储库中，且数据集表示主要以人类使用为导向，缺乏面向智能体的结构化和可操作性设计。

Method: 作者定义了结构化的SciDSK规范，将数据集描述、科学背景、文件组织、使用流程、质量检查和溯源信息集成在一起，同时将底层数据保留在原始存储库中。开发了一个系统化的构建流水线，将每个SciDSK基于权威数据集记录和配套材料进行锚定。建立了科学数据技能库平台，覆盖六个科学学科，支持包访问、持久标识和源数据集可追溯性。

Result: 通过数据集发现检索基准和受控的数据集解读案例进行评估，结果显示SciDSK显著提升了智能体驱动的数据集发现效果，并为数据集解读提供了更精确、可操作的支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果验证了以智能体就绪的表示形式组织数据集特定知识的价值，为科学数据与AI智能体的有效衔接提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Scientific data are increasingly used by AI agents, yet existing dataset representations provide limited support for autonomous discovery, interpretation, and invocation. This limitation stems from the fragmentation of scientific data across heterogeneous repositories and from dataset representations designed primarily for human use. To address this limitation, we introduce the Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK), an agent-ready representation that packages dataset-specific knowledge and operational guidance as a reusable agent skill. A SciDSK integrates dataset descriptions, scientific context, file organization, usage procedures, quality checks, and provenance information while retaining the underlying data in its original repository. We define a structured SciDSK specification and develop a systematic construction pipeline that grounds each SciDSK in authoritative dataset records and associated supporting materials. We further establish the Scientific Data Skill Bank, a unified platform that publishes SciDSK resources across six scientific disciplines and supports package access, persistent identification, and traceability to source datasets. We evaluate SciDSK through a retrieval benchmark for dataset discovery and controlled cases for dataset interpretation. The results show that SciDSK improves agent-driven dataset discovery and provides more precise and actionable support for dataset interpretation. These findings support the value of organizing dataset-specific knowledge in an agent-ready representation.

</details>


### [96] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态跟踪（state tracking）作为LLM智能体记忆系统的新能力，构建了StateMemBench基准（234个多会话场景），并设计了StateMem状态优先记忆方法。StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上使当前状态准确率提升1.8倍（0.205→0.363），在Qwen-3.5-9B上提升1.6倍（0.149→0.233），并可作为轻量级单次调用包装器提升六种记忆/检索后端准确率+32~+67点，其中+15~+32点归因于状态结构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准多聚焦于回忆型任务（recall-shaped tasks），但实际部署中LLM智能体需要在长时间、高价值任务中跟踪不断演化的世界状态——事实、约束和决策会被修订，答案必须反映当前状态而非已被取代的状态。现有记忆系统、检索增强基线和长上下文基线在此能力上均表现不佳，存在关键空白。

Method: 1) 定义状态跟踪能力并构建StateMemBench基准，包含234个多会话场景、两种对话长度区间；采用封闭池评分区分当前状态、被取代状态或失败，从构造上将状态跟踪失败与其他错误分离。2) 提出StateMem状态优先记忆方法，显式跟踪状态取代（supersession）和关系依赖（relational dependencies）。3) 将状态方法作为轻量级单次调用包装器应用于现有记忆系统，并设计了长度和成本匹配的对照实验以分离状态结构贡献与额外上下文贡献。

Result: StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上比最强同骨干基线提升当前状态准确率1.8倍（0.205→0.363），比最强记忆系统在Qwen-3.5-9B上提升1.6倍（0.149→0.233），同时与长上下文基线保持竞争力。作为包装器应用于六种记忆/检索后端时，当前状态准确率提升+32~+67点；长度与成本匹配的对照实验中，+15~+32点归因于状态结构而非额外上下文。

Conclusion: 状态跟踪是记忆系统中独立于回忆能力的关键维度，现有系统在此能力上显著不足。StateMem显式建模状态取代与关系依赖可大幅提升当前状态准确率，且其核心思想可轻量移植到已有记忆系统上产生显著收益，说明状态结构本身是提升记忆系统有效性的重要且可控的因素。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed for longer and higher-stakes tasks, their memory systems continue to have crucial gaps. While existing memory benchmarks focus largely on recall-shaped tasks, we argue an effective memory system must track the evolving state of the world; as facts, constraints, and decisions are revised over a long interaction, answers must reflect the current state and not a superseded one. We define this capability as state tracking and instantiate it in StateMemBench, a benchmark of 234 multi-session scenarios spanning two conversation-length regimes. Its closed-pool grading scores whether an answer reflects the current state, the superseded state, or fails otherwise, separating state-tracking failures from other errors by construction. Our analysis shows that this task is challenging for existing memory systems, retrieval-augmented baselines, and long-context baselines. We then present StateMem, a state-first memory method that explicitly tracks supersession and relational dependencies, and show it improves current-state accuracy over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 1.8x (0.205 -> 0.363) on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and over the strongest memory system by 1.6x (0.149 -> 0.233) on Qwen-3.5-9B, while remaining competitive with the long-context baselines. Finally, we show the same state approach can be applied as a lightweight single-call wrapper over existing memory systems, lifting current-state accuracy by +32 to +67 points on StateMemBench across six memory and retrieval backends. A length- and cost-matched control attributes +15 to +32 of those points to state structure rather than added context.

</details>


### [97] [Frequency-Aware Continual Learning for Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19680)
*Tenghui Huang,Jiawen Kang,Dongning Liu,Changyan Yi,Chengjun Cai,Anjia Yang,Li Li,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段框架来应对LLM智能合约漏洞检测中三个因果关联的挑战：FA-LoRA（频域低秩自适应）实现参数高效适配，FAR（遗忘感知重放）缓解灾难性遗忘，APPM（锚点保护渐进合并）将多适配器整合为单一模型。在DIVE数据集上实验验证，APPM合并后Micro-F1达0.8085，仅比独立任务上界低2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能合约漏洞检测面临三个相互关联的挑战：新漏洞类别需要参数高效适配（全量重训成本过高）；在共享骨干上逐任务训练适配器会导致灾难性遗忘；推理时任务身份未知，必须将多个适配器整合为单一模型。这三个挑战的因果关系要求一个整体集成框架来解决。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：(1) 适配阶段使用FA-LoRA，在傅里叶域进行适配并引入逐频率重要性门控，仅需0.4%可训练参数；(2) 持续学习阶段采用FAR，利用频率门控通过损失动态估计逐样本遗忘风险并优先重放易遗忘的漏洞知识；(3) 部署阶段采用APPM，利用FAR训练产生的非对称泛化特性识别最强泛化适配器作为锚点，通过锚点保护的加权合并与频域门控竞争将所有适配器整合为单模型。

Result: FA-LoRA以0.4%的可训练参数在性能上超越标准LoRA和QLoRA；FAR在顺序任务上平均Micro-F1达0.8022；APPM实现Micro-F1为0.8085，与独立逐任务上界仅差2.7%，合并成本156ms且无额外运行时内存开销。在DIVE数据集上的实验验证了框架对三种挑战的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将参数高效适配、无遗忘持续学习和适配器合并三个相互依赖的环节统一起来，为不断演化的区块链生态中的智能合约漏洞检测提供了一套完整的解决方案，且在性能、参数效率和推理开销方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerability detection with Large Language Models (LLMs) faces three causally linked challenges. First, new vulnerability categories demand parameter-efficient adaptation, since full retraining is prohibitive for sequentially arriving tasks. Second, training per-task adapters on a shared backbone causes catastrophic forgetting of previously learned vulnerabilities. Third, the resulting multiplicity of adapters must be consolidated into a single model, since task identity is unknown at inference time. Each challenge arises directly from the solution to its predecessor, making an integrated framework essential. We propose a three-stage pipeline in which each stage addresses one challenge and feeds into the next. The adaptation stage uses Frequency-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation (FA-LoRA), which performs adaptation in the Fourier domain with per-frequency importance gates, requiring only 0.4% trainable parameters while outperforming standard LoRA and QLoRA. The continual learning stage applies Forget-Aware Replay (FAR), which uses these frequency gates to estimate per-sample forgetting risk via loss dynamics and prioritizes vulnerable knowledge for rehearsal, achieving an average Micro-F1 of 0.8022 across sequential tasks. The deployment stage employs Anchor-Protected Progressive Merging (APPM), which exploits the asymmetric generalization produced by FAR training to identify the strongest-generalizing adapter as an anchor and consolidates all adapters into a single model via anchor-protected weighted merging with frequency-domain gate competition. APPM achieves a Micro-F1 of 0.8085, within 2.7% of the independent per-task upper bound, at a merge cost of 156 ms and no additional runtime memory. Experiments on DIVE confirm the framework effectively addresses all three challenges for evolving blockchain ecosystems.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Hierarchical Skill Policies with Offline Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19684)
*Tanachai Anakewat,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QDOS提出了一种统一的离线到在线学习流水线，通过优势加权质量多样性预训练目标来从数据集中提取多样化且高价值的技能，并结合双数据集复用策略，显著提升强化学习在稀疏奖励任务中的样本效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于两阶段策略的技能提取方法（如轨迹VAE）的缺点是低层策略的质量高度依赖于数据集的原始质量，当数据集质量不佳时，提取的技能质量也会受限，从而影响后续任务的解决效果。

Method: QDOS包含两个核心组件：(1) 优势加权质量多样性（Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity）预训练目标，根据每个轨迹片段的估计优势对技能提取和多样性目标进行加权，从而提取多样化且高价值的技能；(2) 双数据集复用策略，将离线数据既用于技能预训练，又通过伪标签方式填充在线回放缓冲区。

Result: 在结构化操作任务和非结构化运动任务上的实验表明，QDOS显著优于强基线方法，能够加速探索并提升挑战性稀疏奖励领域的最终回报。

Conclusion: QDOS通过优势加权质量多样性预训练和双数据集复用策略，提供了稳健且任务相关的技能表征，有效解决了低层策略质量依赖数据集质量的问题，验证了其在稀疏奖励领域中的优越性能。

Abstract: Recent studies investigate how to leverage pre-collected datasets to improve the policy performance and sample efficiency of RL. One promising approach to achieve this goal is to employ a two-stage strategy: In the first stage, diverse skills are extracted as a low-level policy from a given dataset, and a high-level policy is trained to solve a specific task in the second stage. Typically, extraction of the low-level policy is performed based on unsupervised learning such as trajectory VAE. However, a limitation of this approach is that the quality of the low-level policy highly depends on the quality of the dataset. To address this issue, we introduce QDOS (Quality-Diversity Offline Skill learning), a unified pipeline for robust offline-to-online learning. Our approach incorporates an Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity pretraining objective, which weights the skill extraction and diversity objectives by the estimated advantage of each trajectory segment. This approach allows the model to extract diverse and high-value skills. By providing robust and task-relevant skill representations, QDOS significantly improves the quality of the embedded skill space used by the low-level policy. We further integrate this with a dual dataset reuse strategy, where offline data is used both for skill pretraining and for populating the online replay buffer via pseudo-labeling. Experiments demonstrate that QDOS significantly outperforms strong baselines in structured manipulation tasks and unstructured locomotion tasks, confirming its ability to accelerate exploration and improve final returns in challenging sparse-reward domains.

</details>


### [99] [Rethinking the Evaluation and Optimization of LLM-Based Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19689)
*Pei Wang,Xu Chen,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵的主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient）来量化人类行为模拟任务的主观程度，并据此提出主观性自适应软标签训练（SALT）方法，将语义相近输入的输出聚合为软分布标签，聚合半径随主观性动态调整。同时构建了SUBJSIM基准（19,300个场景、193位标注者、100个主观问题）用于分布级评估，实验证明该方法优于传统基于准确率的评估和硬标签训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM社会模拟是对传统调查和行为实验的有力补充，但其保真度评估存在根本性问题：现行实践以"准确率"为标准，即检查模型是否选择了人类观察到的唯一响应，并训练LLM复现该硬标签。然而人类行为本质上是主观的——同一人在同一情境下可能合理地产出不同行为，观察到的响应只是潜在响应分布中的一次采样，这导致准确率评估不可靠、硬标签训练具有误导性。

Method: 1) 提出主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient），一种基于熵的量化指标，用于区分客观任务（如编码）与主观任务（如社会模拟），并系统分析准确率评估和硬标签训练如何随主观性增长而失效。2) 提出Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training (SALT)：将语义相近输入的输出池化为软分布标签，聚合半径根据每个输入的主观性估计自适应调整；在近客观极限下邻域收缩，自然退化为标准单标签训练。3) 构建SUBJSIM基准：19,300个上下文，覆盖193位标注者和100个主观问题，用于分布级评估。

Result: 在SUBJSIM基准上的实验表明，SALT方法优于传统方法。由于现实数据通常每个输入只有单一观测，实验从单一观测输出训练模型、却针对完整响应分布进行评估，验证了该方法在现实场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于主观性系数的分析揭示了准确率评估和硬标签训练在主观任务中的固有缺陷，SALT方法通过主观性自适应的软标签策略有效解决了这一问题。SUBJSIM基准为后续分布级评估提供了基础，证明在仅有单一观测的现实条件下也能提升LLM社会模拟的保真度。

Abstract: LLM-based social simulation is a promising complement to traditional methods such as surveys and behavioral experiments. A core question is how to evaluate the fidelity of LLM-simulated human behavior and optimize LLMs toward it. Prevailing practice evaluates by accuracy, checking whether the model selects the single response observed from a human, and trains the LLM to reproduce this hard label.
